Method: 采用残差化（residualization）方法，将音素信息从表征中部分剔除，从而分离出独立于局部语音内容的成分。将该方法应用于HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0的表征，检验其是否编码了与音素无关的词信息，并将此解耦方法用于词发现任务。

Result: 在较深层中，HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0总体上确实学到了能以合理保真度编码词的表征，且该编码独立于局部语音内容。这种简单的解耦方法能够增强词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。

Conclusion: 词区分能力不足以证明模型具有专门的词表征，通过残差化分离音素信息后，可证明高层表征中存在与形式无关的词信息；该解耦手段对提升词发现等高阶语言任务有效。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech foundation models are now used in a wide array of downstream applications, including traditional speech recognition and as the basis for tokens in speech-aware language models. Attempts to understand their usefulness have largely focused on probing their representations' ability to discriminate phonemes and words. However, discriminative ability for words need not imply specialized representation of words per se. Good discrimination of words may be explained by good encoding of word form (phonemes) rather than form-independent word representations encoding identity or syntactic/semantic properties. By partialling out phoneme information using residualization, we show that, in later layers, HuBERT and wav2vec 2.0 do in general learn representations which encode words with reasonable fidelity independently of local phonetic content. We show that this simple approach to disentanglement can enhance higher-order linguistic information in word discovery tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Building Multilingual Bridges: Data Mixing as the Pillar of Generalization for In-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10445)
*Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Ananya Sahu,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Daniel D'souza,Alexandre Berard,Thomas Euyang,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出L2推理能力（按用户提示语言进行推理），通过优化SFT数据组成与调度，在3.35B规模模型上实现跨60种语言超过93%的L2推理率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型高度英语中心化，无论提示语言如何都主要以英语推理，这对非英语用户不友好，可能丢失原问题意图，也放弃了目标语言更易表达的知识。

Method: 从数据中心化角度出发，研究SFT中数据组成与调度对推理泛化（尤其是L2推理）的优化方法，构建3.35B规模模型Tiny Aya L2-Thinker，在6个基准（涵盖数学、常识推理、指令遵循、开放式生成和文化推理）上评估。

Result: 在60种语言上实现超过93%的L2推理率，同时保持较强性能；并表明更广泛的语言覆盖、现成的多语言非推理数据以及足够的英语推理骨干能帮助泛化到未训练语言。

Conclusion: 推理是一种语言无关的行为，可通过精心数据混合跨类型多样的语言迁移，无需每种目标语言的推理监督。作者发布模型权重和多语言推理数据以支持可访问的本地语言推理研究。

Abstract: Reasoning language models have made substantial advances on a variety of complex tasks, yet their capabilities remain overwhelmingly English-centric: models primarily reason in English regardless of the language they are prompted in. This is inaccessible for non-English-speaking users, risks losing the intent of the original question, and forgoes knowledge more readily expressed in the target language. In this work, we advance L2 reasoning, the ability of a model to reason consistently in the language of the user's prompt, thus building an in-language bridge between the prompt and the answer. We approach this problem from a data-centric angle, investigating how to optimize data composition and scheduling in SFT for reasoning generalization. Building Tiny Aya L2-Thinker at 3.35B scale, we achieve an L2 reasoning rate above 93% across 60 languages on 6 benchmarks spanning math, commonsense reasoning, instruction following, open-ended generation, and cultural reasoning while keeping performance strong. We show the path to generalizing L2 reasoning to held-out languages goes through broader language coverage, readily available multilingual non-reasoning data, and a sufficient English reasoning backbone. These findings indicate that reasoning is a language-agnostic behavior that can be transferred across typologically diverse languages through careful data mixing and without requiring reasoning supervision in every target language. We release our model weights and multilingual reasoning data to support further research on accessible, in-language reasoning.

</details>


### [145] [IBIB: A Protocol for Measuring Enterprise AI Systems by Serving Route, Not Model Identifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10494)
*Blake Stenstrom,Charangan Vasantharajan,Brian Sathianathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文认为现有评测把"可用能力"简化为模型标识符打分是测量误差，提出 IB2 协议使该误差可报告：包含绑定预检、含可靠性的首轮评分与结构性盲评三部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业实际部署的是系统而非单个检查点，可用能力由权重、服务路由、精度、输出契约与框架共同决定；但 18 个受审基准都只按宣传的模型标识符打分，存在系统性测量误差。

Method: IB2 协议三部分：(1) 金标准盲的能力绑定预检，在任何任务到达前验证路由能否执行评测契约；(2) 可靠性纳入的首轮评分规则，把失败保留在分数中同时排除不受支持的能力；(3) 结构性分数盲的裁决。发布算法、分类表、请求契约与清单模式，参考实例为 128 个锁定任务、987 条断言，覆盖文档、表格、图表、工具与数据库工作，语料保持封存。

Result: 十一个系统上四项结果：能力可用性可测（两次完整单路由运行后来分别未通过绑定门的不同谓词，第三次在重跑前通过该门，而宣传标识符未暴露此限制）；区分度不均匀（七套件中四套在六系统带内饱和，差异几乎全部来自受治理数据库工作与多表连接，故报告区间支撑的分辨组而非排名；五标签输出的四个切点未通过多重性校正）；服务臂选择使某一声明版本与精度从 77.38 变为 82.54，配对区间 [0.11,10.60]，但各臂在访问模式、框架代次与服务工具调用解析器上不同，且框架代次属于评测器属性而非端点属性；将失败响应排除出分母会改变点排序，说明可靠性纳入改变的是结论而非措辞。

Conclusion: 能力评测应把系统级绑定、可靠性与盲评纳入协议，程序本身即为可交付的产物；报告应以区间支撑的分辨组替代排名，并保留失败响应以避免结论被分母口径操控。

Abstract: Enterprises deploy systems, not checkpoints. Usable capability depends jointly on weights, serving route, precision, output contract, and harness, yet all 18 audited benchmarks score advertised model identifiers. We treat this as measurement error and give a protocol that makes it reportable. It has three parts. A gold-blind capability-binding preflight verifies that a route can execute the evaluation contract before any task reaches it; a reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule keeps failure in the score while keeping unsupported capability out; and adjudication is structurally score-blind. We call the protocol IB2 and release its algorithms, classification tables, request contract, and manifest schemas. Its reference instantiation, 128 locked tasks and 987 assertions over document, spreadsheet, chart, tool and database work, stays sealed: the procedure is the artifact, not the corpus. Across eleven systems, four results. Capability availability is measurable: two complete single-route runs on identical weights later failed distinct predicates of the finalized binding gate, while a third passed that gate before a fresh run. The advertised identifier exposed neither limit. Discrimination is not uniform: four of seven suites saturate under a six-system band, with the spread almost entirely from governed database work and multi-tab joins, so we report interval-backed resolution groups, not ranks; two of the nominal five-label output's four cuts fail multiplicity adjustment. Serving-arm choice moved one declared revision and precision from 77.38 to 82.54, paired interval [0.11,10.60], though the arms differ in access mode, harness generation, and the serving tool-call parser, and harness generation is a property of our evaluator, not any endpoint. Excluding failed responses from denominators changes the point ordering, so reliability inclusion changes a conclusion, not its wording.

</details>


### [146] [IdeaAMBIG: Benchmarking Implementation-Critical Gaps in Research-Idea Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10539)
*Yiling Ma,Yilun Zhao,Sihong Wu,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究面向实现的研究方法规格的"可编码就绪度"，提出IdeaAMBIG基准（660个实例），评估大模型的缺陷定位与澄清动作生成能力，发现缺陷定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究想法可能新颖合理，但所提方法往往描述不足，难以被忠实实现。论文关注"研究方法规格是否提供足够信息，使有能力的实现者或编码代理无需无据假设即可构建目标方法"，即"可编码就绪度"问题。

Method: 从论文、代码库、issue讨论串和复现产物中构建有证据支撑的规格及其已支持解析结果。提出IdeaAMBIG基准，含660个有证据支撑的实例：163个来自可复现性报告与GitHub issue的真实缺陷缺口，497个注入到可编码就绪参考中的受控合成缺口。评估三项能力：可编码就绪度评估、缺陷定位、澄清动作生成。缺陷定位仅给定规格，澄清任务额外给定已标注缺陷。

Result: 在13个LLM上，最佳模型在真实实例上的宏缺陷恢复率仅9.6%，但在给定缺陷时宏澄清动作成功率可达80.6%。在oracle研究中，提供金标准解析可将下游可编码就绪率从14%提升至98%。

Conclusion: 在所有评估模型中，缺陷定位是主要瓶颈；一旦给定缺陷，模型的澄清能力更强。弥补规格歧义有望显著提升方法的可编码与可复现性。

Abstract: A research idea may be novel, coherent, and scientifically plausible, yet its proposed method may remain insufficiently specified for faithful implementation. We study the codification readiness of implementation-facing research-method specifications, defined by whether they provide sufficient methodological information for a competent implementer or coding agent to construct the intended method without unsupported assumptions. We construct evidence-grounded specifications and their supported resolutions from papers, codebases, issue threads, and reproduction artifacts. We introduce IdeaAMBIG, a benchmark of 660 evidence-grounded instances: 163 real-world gaps from reproducibility reports and GitHub issues, and 497 controlled synthetic gaps injected into codification-ready references. IdeaAMBIG evaluates three capabilities: codification-readiness assessment, defect localization, and clarification action generation. Defect localization receives only the specification, whereas clarification additionally receives the annotated defect. Across 13 LLMs, the best model achieves 9.6% Macro Defect Recovery Rate on real-world instances but 80.6% Macro Clarification Action Success Rate when given the defect. In an oracle study, supplying the gold resolution raises the downstream codification-ready rate from 14% to 98%. Across all evaluated models, defect localization is the main bottleneck, with stronger clarification given the defect.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Scores Alone Do Not Prove Discovery: The Discovery Certification Protocol for Auditing AI Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09219)
*Jingjie Ning,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"发现认证协议"（DCP），通过可执行的恢复测试与反馈测试，对AI研究智能体的结果声明进行分级认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究智能体结合先验知识、公开来源与实验反馈产出结果，但缺乏统一的证据标准来判定结果是否真正有用、是否存在替代路径、以及反馈效应是否可信，因此需要一个共同的证据语言。

Method: DCP将结果声明转为可执行的恢复与反馈测试。Gate 1在密封评测上验证有用改进；Gate 2向匹配智能体提供注册的起始信息与观测到的网页内容、但隐藏目标研究历史，任何达标方法须提供恢复见证并触发Core否决；DCP Core要求充分对照、零观测恢复，并在一个全新注册回合上给出恢复的有限样本界；可选Gate 3在共享检查点上测量真实反馈相对指定中性策略的平均效应，DCP Evidence在独立零校准与注册效应边际之后纳入该效应。另设确定性、无LLM的验证器。

Result: 在SQLite优化与虚拟催化剂控制两项受控审计中（使用不同模型），96个回合均零恢复，上界0.0468；每项配对研究得到30次真实恢复与0次中性恢复，并通过60对零假设研究；额外案例覆盖Core、已恢复与审计不完整三类决策；确定性无LLM验证器可从冻结证据复现全部决策。

Conclusion: DCP为AI研究中的有用成果、替代路径与反馈效应提供了统一的证据语言与可复现的认证流程。

Abstract: AI research agents combine prior knowledge, public sources, and experimental feedback to produce useful results. The Discovery Certification Protocol (DCP) turns claims about these results into executable recovery and feedback tests. Gate 1 validates useful improvement on sealed evaluation. Gate 2 gives matched agents the registered starting information and observed Web content while withholding the target research history. Every valid method reaching the numerical target supplies a recovery witness and triggers the Core veto. DCP Core requires adequate controls, zero observed recoveries, and a finite-sample bound on recovery in one fresh registered episode. Optional Gate 3 measures the average effect of truthful feedback relative to a specified neutral policy from a shared checkpoint. DCP Evidence adds this effect after independent null calibration and a registered effect margin. Two controlled audits exercise the complete protocol in SQLite optimization and virtual catalyst control under different models. Each produced zero recoveries in 96 episodes, with an upper bound of 0.0468. Each paired study yielded 30 truthful recoveries and zero neutral recoveries, with passing 60-pair null studies. Additional cases exercise Core, recovered, and audit-incomplete decisions. A deterministic, LLM-free verifier reproduces the decisions from frozen evidence. DCP provides a common evidence language for useful outcomes, alternative routes, and feedback effects across AI research.

</details>


### [148] [Who Are They to Each Other? Multi-Agent Reasoning for Speaker Relationship Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09628)
*Yaohan Guan,Yen-Ju Lu,Yuzhe Wang,Junhyeok Lee,Jesus Villalba,Laureano Moro Velazquez,Thomas Thebaud,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的多智能体推理框架，通过LLM智能体之间的结构化交互来推断对话者关系，并设计了两种方案：多角色多智能体辩论和多智能体竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从口语对话中推断说话者关系是社交感知语音理解的重要一步，但该任务研究不足，监督式建模训练成本高、难以扩展；现有推理时LLM方法缺乏结构，难以处理细微、分布式、多模态且可能存在多种合理解释的关系线索。

Method: 提出免训练的多智能体推理框架，通过LLM智能体的结构化交互组织推断，使关系判断可被提出、质疑和裁决，无需任务专属训练。实例化两种设计：(1) 多角色多智能体辩论，为智能体分配互补角色或基于社会理论的视角；(2) 多智能体竞争，通过成对裁决比较判断、淘汰较弱候选并保留最具辩护力的结果。

Result: 在Seamless Interaction数据集上跨不同模态设置评估，涵盖二分类和细粒度关系细节预测。结果表明多数情况下优于零样本和现有多智能体基线。人工评估显示该任务对人类也很困难：LLM方法在含文本设置中有时可超过人工标注者，但在音频设置中竞争力较弱。

Conclusion: 关系推断受益于智能体间的结构化推理时交互，但当前模型尚未充分捕捉声学线索。

Abstract: Inferring speaker relationships from spoken conversations is an important step towards socially aware speech understanding. However, this task remains underexplored, and supervised modeling is costly to train and scale. At the same time, existing inference-time LLM approaches provide limited structure for handling subtle, distributed, and multimodal relational cues that may support multiple plausible interpretations. To address these limitations, we introduce a training-free multi-agent reasoning framework that organizes inference through structured interaction among LLM agents, allowing relationship judgments to be proposed, challenged, and adjudicated without task-specific training. We instantiate this framework with two complementary designs. We propose Multi-Role Multi-Agent Debate as a task-specific adaptation of standard multi-agent debate for speaker relationship inference, assigning agents complementary roles or social-theory-grounded perspectives rather than a single undifferentiated viewpoint. In contrast, we introduce Multi-Agent Compete, a competition-based protocol that compares agent judgments through pairwise adjudication, eliminates weaker candidates, and retains the most defensible one. We evaluate these methods on the Seamless Interaction dataset across different modality settings, covering both binary classification and fine-grained relationship-detail prediction. Results suggest that they improve over zero-shot and existing multi-agent baselines in most cases. Human evaluation further suggests that this task is challenging even for people. LLM methods can sometimes outperform human annotators in text-included settings but are less competitive in the audio setting. Together, these findings suggest that relationship inference benefits from structured inference-time interaction among agents, while acoustic cues are not yet fully captured by current models.

</details>


### [149] [Glyph: A Multi-Strategy Agentic System for Column Description and Sensitivity-Ontology Tagging of Enterprise Data Catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10430)
*Kostia Kudriavtsev,Parvez Rafi,Sha Sundaram*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Glyph 是一个生产系统，将列描述生成与列类型标注作为两个耦合问题，用状态图编排的 LLM 智能体协作完成。描述智能体基于生产该列的管道源代码（通过主动 RAG 从企业 GitHub 按需检索）生成描述；标注智能体并行运行三种策略并采用 RRF 融合，实现可审计、可运维的数据目录服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据湖的表增长速度超过人工维护能力，导致列描述缺失、治理标签未分配，形成"文档债务"，进而损害数据发现、访问控制与合规性。现有列类型标注工作及商业价值/正则敏感扫描器存在局限，需要一种值无关、代码接地、带来源追踪且可优雅降级的方案。

Method: 1) Descriptor 智能体：以生产该列的管道源代码为生成依据，通过推理-行动工具循环（主动 RAG）按需从企业 GitHub 检索。2) Tagger 智能体：基于受治理的 275 叶数据分类本体，并行运行三种互补策略（描述标注器、业务线正则标注器、由微调对比编码器加向量库支持的元数据标注器），再用 Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) 融合排序输出。3) 微调 6 层 MiniLM 元数据编码器，采用批内对比目标。

Result: 微调后同标签检索在同分布留出集上显著提升：NDCG@10 由 0.55 升至 0.92，MAP@100 由 0.19 升至 0.90。端到端多标签标注质量在召回加权的 F2 目标下于三个评估组报告，并给出隔离各策略及 RRF 融合的消融实验。

Conclusion: Glyph 以值无关、代码接地、逐标签来源可溯及优雅降级的设计区别于既有列类型标注工作和商业扫描器，使多智能体 LLM 目录编目可作为生产服务被审计与运维。

Abstract: Enterprise data lakes accumulate tables faster than human stewards can document or classify them, leaving columns with missing descriptions and unassigned governance labels. This documentation debt undermines data discovery, access control, and regulatory compliance. We present Glyph, a production system that frames two coupled problems, column description generation and column type annotation for data classification, as cooperating LLM agents orchestrated as stateful graphs. The Descriptor grounds generation in the pipeline source code that produces each column, retrieved on demand from an enterprise GitHub via a reasoning--acting tool loop (active Retrieval-Augmented Generation). The Tagger assigns labels from a governed 275-leaf Data Classification Ontology by running three complementary strategies in parallel (a description tagger, a line-of-business regex tagger, and a metadata tagger backed by a fine-tuned contrastive encoder over a vector database), then fuses their ranked outputs with Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF). We fine-tune a 6-layer MiniLM metadata encoder with an in-batch contrastive objective, lifting same-tag retrieval on an in-distribution held-out split from NDCG@10 0.55 to 0.92 (MAP@100 $0.19 \rightarrow 0.90$) relative to the stock base encoder. We report end-to-end multi-label tagging quality under a recall-weighted F2 objective across three evaluation groups, an ablation isolating each strategy and the RRF fusion, and the engineering decisions that distinguish Glyph from prior column-type-annotation work and from commercial value/regex sensitivity scanners: value-free and code-grounded design, per-tag provenance, and graceful degradation. Together these make multi-agent LLM cataloging auditable and operable as a production service.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [OASIS: A Rubric-Based Multimodal Assessment Platform Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09180)
*Ameer H. Shakur,Shinyoung Kang,David Hein,Michael J. Holcomb,Huong-Tra Ngo,Aarash Zakeri,Minhan Park,Licheng Yi,Dhanush Jain,Hunter Schuler,Andrew R. Jamieson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OASIS 是一个面向规模化评分的基础设施平台，用大语言模型对视频、音频和文本进行基于量规的评分。它将独立命令行工具与 Elephant + MAPLES 集成栈结合，支持本地或托管模型，并已在真实医疗教育场景中投入生产使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次使用大模型评分较为简单，但要在规模上部署评估，需要处理遭遇管理、量规版本控制、多模态执行、溯源记录与人工复核等一系列系统性问题。论文旨在提供一套支撑大规模、可审计的多模态评分基础设施。

Method: OASIS 将独立 CLI 与规范的 Elephant + MAPLES 集成栈配对，用于遭遇管理和多模态评分编排。系统可对接托管 API，也可通过 Ollama 和兼容 OpenAI 的端点（如 vLLM）调用自托管开放权重模型。可选扩展包括 SimRubrics 量规编写和 Wayfinder 会话式智能体网关，二者使用与人工操作者相同的认证接口。给定量规和录制的遭遇后，系统产出逐条评分、证据与理由，并保留执行工件以供审计。

Result: 系统自 2023 年秋季起在 UT Southwestern Medical Center 投入生产，已处理超过 7,000 次遭遇。特色功能包括量规即程序编译、渐进式执行计划、内容可寻址的评分身份、转录增强的多模态评分、显式复核状态，以及面向人类与自主智能体的共享命令界面。

Conclusion: 尽管诞生于医学教育领域，OASIS 的架构具有领域无关性，适用于任何可从录制或书面工件中评估结构化表现的场景。本文仅包含报告与项目信息，不包含应用源代码、二进制文件、安装材料、示例数据或带标签的软件版本。

Abstract: OASIS (Open Assessment and Scoring Infrastructure Stack) is a systems platform for rubric-based grading of video, audio, and text with large language models. Scoring one artifact with an LLM is straightforward; deploying assessment at scale requires encounter management, rubric versioning, modality-aware execution, provenance capture, and human review. OASIS pairs a standalone command-line interface with a canonical integrated Elephant + MAPLES stack for encounter management and multimodal grading orchestration. Both paths can target hosted APIs or self-hosted open-weight models through Ollama and OpenAI-compatible endpoints such as vLLM. SimRubrics rubric authoring and the Wayfinder conversational agent gateway are optional extensions that use the same authenticated interfaces as human operators. Given a rubric and recorded encounters, OASIS produces per-criterion scores, evidence, and rationales, preserving execution artifacts for audit. Distinctive features include rubric-as-program compilation, progressive execution plans, content-addressable grading identity, transcript-augmented multimodal grading, explicit review state, and a shared command surface for humans and autonomous agents. Though developed in medical education, the architecture is domain-agnostic, applying wherever structured performance can be evaluated from recorded or written artifacts. In production at UT Southwestern Medical Center since Fall 2023, the platform has processed more than 7,000 encounters. This publication includes the report and project information, not application source, binaries, installation materials, sample data, or a tagged software release.

</details>


### [151] [Evaluating Enterprise Analytics Agents: An End-to-End, Trace-Backed Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09182)
*Teja Venkat Kolli,Sang Su Lee,Xueying Yan,Jessie Chen,Chi Cheng,Kartik Ravisankar,Shishir Dash,Vijay Anand Raghavan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出针对企业分析智能体（analytics agents）的端到端评测方法论，从语义理解、执行质量、可靠性三个维度对智能体的行为进行分级评分，而非只评估最终答案。在大型在线市场的内部智能体上进行案例验证（50个问题、2种配置、每种3次重复，共300条轨迹），揭示出高能力配置虽减少了拒答、增加了真实数据回答，但也带来工具预算耗尽、模式探索超支和表格解释不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析智能体并非简单的 text-to-SQL 系统：它们要理解业务意图、选择指标定义、选定数据源、执行工具并生成自然语言答案，这些答案可能影响运营、财务或高管决策。仅对最终答案打分掩盖了失败位置——看似合理的答案可能使用了错误的真相来源、跳过必要分解、无依据地声称因果关系，或在多次运行中改变表格解释。因此需要端到端、基于轨迹的评测方法。

Method: 提出端到端评测方法论，对智能体行为按三大类进行评分：（1）语义理解；（2）执行质量；（3）可靠性。评分依据包含人工撰写金标准答案的题库、重复运行以及运行时轨迹。每次运行先通过"运行有效性检查"，再获得分级的、考虑弃答（abstention-aware）的分数，这些分数输入决策框架而非作为发布门槛。在大型在线市场的受控内部分析智能体上进行实例化：使用50个分析问题、两种匿名模型配置、每种配置3次随机重复，共产生300条轨迹。

Result: 高能力配置将早期拒答率从73%降至0%，真实数据回答率从21%提升至73%；但在16%的运行中耗尽工具轮次预算，在77%的轨迹中超出模式探索预算，并在50个问题中的41个问题上改变了表格解释。在具有结构化金标准答案的财务问题上，源表使用和升级（escalation）有所改善，但规范分解（canonical decomposition）在两种配置中均表现较弱。

Conclusion: 对分析智能体的信任需要端到端的、基于轨迹的评测，而不能仅依赖 SQL 正确性或最终答案质量。该方法论通过多维度、多轮次的评分与决策框架，能够暴露仅看最终答案时被掩盖的失败模式。

Abstract: Enterprise analytics agents are not only text-to-SQL systems. They interpret business intent and choose metric definitions. They select data sources, execute tools, inspect results, and produce natural-language answers. Those answers may influence operational, financial, or executive decisions. Grading final answers hides where these agents fail. A plausible answer can use the wrong source of truth. It can skip a required decomposition, claim causality without support, or change its table interpretation across repeated runs. We present an end-to-end evaluation methodology for analytics agents. The methodology grades agent behavior across three families: semantic understanding, execution quality, and reliability. Grading uses question banks with human-written golden answers, repeated runs, and runtime traces. Each run first passes a run-validity check, then receives tiered, abstention-aware scores that feed a decision framework rather than a release gate. We instantiate the methodology on a controlled internal analytics agent at a large online marketplace. The case study uses 50 analytics questions, two anonymous model configurations, and three randomized repetitions per configuration, yielding 300 traces. The higher-capability configuration reduced early refusal from 73% to 0% and increased real-data answers from 21% to 73%. However, it also exhausted the tool-round budget on 16% of runs. It overran the schema-exploration budget on 77% of traces. It changed its table interpretation on 41 of 50 questions. On finance questions with structured golden answers, source table use and escalation improved, but canonical decomposition remained weak in both configurations. These results show why trust in analytics agents requires end-to-end, trace-backed evaluation rather than SQL correctness or final answer quality alone.

</details>


### [152] [The Double Measurement Confound in Agent Benchmarks: De-Scaffolding, Ground-Truth Scoring, and Reliability Beyond the Mean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09218)
*Yonghong Zhang,Shadi Motaali,Vu Phong Dinh,Avin Piroutiniya,Jorge E. López de Vergara,Luis de Pedro,Ricardo Correia,Isabel M. Parra,Yong Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文指出LLM智能体基准测试存在双重测量混淆（执行决策由固定scaffold完成、评分标准不反映任务正确性），提出测量理论框架与审计-修复协议，实验表明联合干预可将近乎平坦的排行榜转化为区分均值与鲁棒性的可靠性谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准测试被广泛用于比较LLM并指导部署决策，但只有当基准分数衡量的是模型能力而非评测流程属性时才有意义。现有评测中执行关键决策由固定scaffold而非模型完成，且评分器使用的标准可能不反映任务正确性，导致分数含义不清。

Method: 提出统一双重混淆的测量理论框架，刻画基准分数何时可被解释为模型能力的证据；并据此实例化审计-修复协议：(i) 将执行关键决策从scaffold转移到模型；(ii) 用带种子的真值评分替代基于形状的评估；(iii) 通过最坏情况与尾部风险指标报告均值之外的可靠性。

Result: 在ComtradeBench上，联合干预将原本近乎平坦的排行榜转变为可区分平均性能与跨随机种子鲁棒性的可靠性谱。对现有基准的审计显示，评分器有效性是基准特定的，而scaffold归属在所探查之处均为未受控的维度。

Conclusion: 基准分数应与其scaffolding水平、评分标准及可靠性特征一同解读，为LLM智能体更有效的评测提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are increasingly used to compare large language models (LLMs) and guide deployment decisions, yet benchmark scores are meaningful only if they measure model capability rather than properties of the evaluation pipeline. We identify a double measurement confound: execution-critical decisions are performed by a fixed scaffold instead of the model, while the scorer evaluates outputs using criteria that may not reflect task correctness. We unify these issues within a measurement-theoretic framework that characterizes when benchmark scores can be interpreted as evidence of model capability, and instantiate it with an audit-and-repair protocol that (i) transfers execution-critical decisions from the scaffold to the model, (ii) replaces shape-based evaluation with seeded ground-truth scoring, and (iii) reports reliability beyond the mean through worst-case and tail-risk metrics. Experiments on ComtradeBench show that the joint intervention transforms a nearly flat leaderboard into a reliability spectrum that distinguishes both average performance and robustness across seeds. Applying the audit to existing benchmarks further shows that scorer validity is benchmark-specific, whereas scaffold ownership is an uncontrolled axis wherever we probed it. Our results suggest that benchmark scores should be interpreted together with their scaffolding level, scoring criterion, and reliability profile, providing a practical framework for more valid evaluation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [153] [Cost-Aware Post-Hoc Deferral Under Calibration and Shift: An Environmental AI Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09235)
*Haoran Yu,Lifei Liu,Danping Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 EcoTrust 后验框架，研究冻结分类器延迟决策中置信度校准、非对称错误成本、评审员准确率与支持门控的交互作用；在河流热应激任务上，学习型风险估计器提升了排序能力，但校准置信度规则在分布内成本更低，迁移时门控退化为全部送审且仅在评审完美无约束时最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择延迟策略不仅需要排序不确定样本，还需考虑置信度可能失准、错误成本不对称、评审员会犯错，以及部署数据可能超出校准支持范围等问题，因此需要系统研究这些因素的交互影响。

Method: 提出 EcoTrust 后验框架，通过六组错误风险估计器、类别非对称成本、评审员准确率和可选支持门控，比较自动执行与人工评审。在 Columbia River 热应激测试平台上，对比学习风险估计器、Chow 置信度规则、校准匹配的类别感知置信度估计器，并在 12 个现成后端与 10 个河流站点迁移上评估，辅以成对年块自助法。

Result: 学习估计器将错误排序 AUC 从 0.869 提升至 0.889，但 Chow 置信度规则分布内成本更低（每日 0.416 对 0.567）。12 个后端中，学习风险与校准匹配的类别感知置信度估计器各有 6 个优于原始 Chow，成对年块自助法无法分辨其平均成本差异。迁移至十个站点时，门控标记所有案例并退化为始终评审，仅在评审完美且无约束时于八个站点取得最低成本。

Conclusion: 在单一受控任务上，更丰富的风险信号不一定可靠优于校准后的置信度，检测到外推也不意味着可获得可迁移的个案级排序；研究刻画了该任务的决策边界。

Abstract: Choosing a deferral policy for a frozen classifier requires more than ranking uncertain cases: confidence may be miscalibrated, errors have unequal costs, reviewers can err, and deployment data can leave calibration support. We study these interactions through EcoTrust, a post-hoc framework that compares automatic action with review using a six-group error-risk estimator, class-asymmetric costs, reviewer accuracy, and an optional support gate. On a Columbia River thermal-stress testbed, the learned estimator improves error-ranking area under the receiver operating characteristic curve from 0.869 to 0.889, but Chow's confidence rule has lower in-distribution cost (0.416 versus 0.567 per day). Across 12 off-the-shelf backends, learned risk and a calibration-matched, class-aware confidence estimator each beat raw Chow on six; a paired year-block bootstrap does not resolve their mean cost difference. In transfer to ten river stations, the gate flags every case and becomes an always-review fallback, attaining the lowest cost on eight stations only when review is perfect and unconstrained. These results characterize decision boundaries on one controlled task: richer risk signals do not reliably improve on calibrated confidence, and detected extrapolation does not imply transferable case-level ranking.

</details>


### [154] [Talking to Itself While Coding: What Makes Comments Help Code Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09242)
*Dangfeng Pan,Zhensu Sun,Cenyuan Zhang,David Lo,Xiaoning Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 评论之所以能提升代码生成性能，不在于"评论"这一形式本身，而在于其承载了正确的解题内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在写代码时常生成自然语言评论，这些评论会进入后续代码生成的上下文，但究竟评论的哪些属性影响代码生成性能尚不清楚。作者希望通过观察性分析与受控干预来厘清这一问题。

Method: 在LiveCodeBench上进行观察性分析，检验评论频率与评论意图对pass@1的预测作用；随后用更强源模型撰写的评论块预填（prefill）较弱的接收模型，从而将评论的表面形式与其传达的解题内容分离；并测试多种模型与提示变体。

Result: 评论频率与宽泛的评论意图均无法可靠预测pass@1。来自通过测试的源解的评论使接收模型pass@1平均提升17.2%；描述失败解的评论无可靠增益；针对不同问题的评论使pass@1下降20.8%。多数接收模型无法显著复现外部评论带来的增益，最佳情形也仅恢复24%。

Conclusion: 评论有助于代码生成并非因为它是评论，而是因为它能提供提示无法可靠引出的正确解题内容。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate natural-language comments while writing code, and these comments become part of the context used to generate the code that follows. However, it remains unclear which properties of comments affect code-generation performance. We study this question through observational analyses and controlled interventions. On LiveCodeBench, neither comment frequency nor broad comment intent reliably predicts pass@1. We then prefill weaker recipient models with comment blocks written by stronger source models, allowing us to separate comment surface form from the solution content they convey. Comments from source solutions that pass the tests raise recipient pass@1 by 17.2% on average. In contrast, comments describing failed solutions provide no reliable gain, while comments written for a different problem reduce pass@1 by 20.8%. Finally, across a wide range of models and prompt variants, most recipient models show no significant recovery of the external-comment gain, and the best case recovers only 24%. These results show that comments help code generation not merely because they are comments, but because they can provide correct solution content that prompting cannot reliably elicit.

</details>


### [155] [How effective are traditional test criteria at detecting bugs in large language models generated code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09315)
*Asma Hamidi,Michael Konstantinou,Renzo Degiovanni,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过5个LLM和4个基准的实证研究，评估语句覆盖、分支覆盖和变异测试在检测LLM生成代码故障上的有效性，发现实际故障检出率接近零，主要受限于测试预言（oracle）失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛用于代码生成，传统测试充分性准则（此前多基于人类编写的程序、故障和测试）是否能有效检测LLM引入的故障尚不清楚，因此需要实证研究其在该新场景下的有效性。

Method: 开展实证研究，涵盖5个LLM与4个基准，模拟代码与测试均自动生成的端到端工作流，收集6000多个故障程序实例，评估语句覆盖、分支覆盖和变异测试三种充分性准则的有效性与效率。

Result: 多数LLM引入的故障较易捕获；但困难故障难以被传统覆盖准则或变异准则触发；实际故障检出率极低（常接近零），原因是测试预言无法捕获由生成测试前缀触发的故障行为；提示感知预言可提升检出但整体效果有限；变异测试在触发与检出故障上仅略优于传统覆盖准则，但应用成本明显更高。

Conclusion: 在LLM生成代码的场景下，覆盖与变异准则的实用价值受限，瓶颈在于测试预言的自动化生成；用户仍需人工推敲测试断言，且变异测试的高成本在此情境下的合理性存疑。

Abstract: Test adequacy criteria are widely used to evaluate and guide software testing. Although prior research has extensively examined these criteria using human-written programs, faults, and tests, the increasing adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation raises important questions about their effectiveness in detecting LLM-induced faults. To investigate this, we conduct an empirical study involving 5 LLMs and 4 benchmarks, simulating end-to-end workflows in which both code and tests are automatically generated. We collect 6,000+ faulty program instances and evaluate the effectiveness and efficiency of 3 widely used adequacy criteria: statement coverage, branch coverage, and mutation testing. Our findings reveal several key insights. First, most faults introduced by LLMs are relatively trivial to catch. Second, the challenging faults are difficult to trigger using either traditional coverage-based or mutation-based criteria. Third, actual fault detection rates remain extremely low, often near zero, because test oracles fail to capture faulty behavior triggered by the generated test prefixes, exposing a critical limitation of automated test generation. Fourth, prompt-aware oracles can improve fault detection, but their overall effectiveness remains limited, highlighting the need for users to manually reason about test assertions. We further observe that mutation testing only marginally outperforms traditional coverage criteria in both triggering and detecting faults, raising questions about whether its significantly higher application cost is justified in this context.

</details>


### [156] [The Vibe Shift in Software Engineering: Evaluating AI-Led Conversational Programming for Performance, Cognition, and Responsible Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09560)
*Sales G. Aribe,Louie Jay S. Labastida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究评估"氛围编程"(Vibe Coding)这一AI主导的对话式编程范式，发现其显著提升开发效率（较传统编程快27%、较AI辅助编程快12%），但可维护性下降、安全漏洞增多；据此提出负责任采用的三支柱框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型驱动的自然语言编程范式兴起，学界与业界需明确Vibe Coding在效率、认知负荷与软件质量方面的实际表现，并探讨其负责任采用路径。研究旨在通过与传统编程和AI辅助编程的对比，揭示该范式的收益与权衡。

Method: 采用混合方法设计。30名参与者（专业开发者与高级计算机学生）在三种实验条件下完成等价编程任务。定量数据用描述性统计与重复测量方差分析（ANOVA）处理，定性数据用主题分析；同时采用SUS可用性量表与NASA-TLX认知负荷量表进行测评。

Result: Vibe Coding显著提升开发效率，任务完成时间较传统编程减少27%，较AI辅助编程减少12%；但可维护性指标更低、安全漏洞更多，存在软件质量权衡。可用性评分良好（SUS=71.4），认知负荷中等（NASA-TLX=55.5），表现为句法投入减少但语言推理增加。主题分析识别出信任校准、控制感丧失、认知适应与提示工程策略四个关键构念；控制感丧失与安全风险上升相关，源于AI输出透明度与验证不足。

Conclusion: Vibe Coding能提升生产力，但需批判性监督。研究提出负责任采用的三支柱框架：人机混合集成、人工监督与透明问责、情境感知部署；该范式是软件开发的变革性过渡范式而非终点。

Abstract: This study evaluates Vibe Coding, an emerging AI-led conversational programming paradigm that enables developers to generate software through natural-language interaction with large language models. Using a mixed-methods design, the study assessed performance efficiency, cognitive implications, and responsible adoption in comparison with traditional and AI-assisted coding environments. Thirty participants, including professional developers and advanced computing students, completed equivalent programming tasks under three experimental conditions. Quantitative data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and repeated-measures ANOVA, while qualitative data were examined through thematic analysis. Results show that vibe coding significantly improved development efficiency, reducing task completion time by 27% compared with traditional coding and 12% compared with AI-assisted coding. However, these gains were accompanied by lower maintainability indices and higher security vulnerabilities, indicating trade-offs in software quality. Usability results yielded a good rating (SUS = 71.4), while cognitive workload remained moderate (NASA-TLX = 55.5), reflecting reduced syntactic effort but increased linguistic reasoning. Thematic analysis identified trust calibration, loss of control, cognitive adaptation, and prompt-engineering strategy as key constructs. Notably, perceived loss of control was associated with increased security risks due to reduced transparency and validation of AI-generated outputs. Based on these findings, the study proposes a three-pillar framework for responsible adoption: hybrid integration of human and AI capabilities, human oversight and transparent accountability, and context-aware deployment. Overall, vibe coding enhances productivity but requires critical oversight, reinforcing its role as a transformative yet transitional paradigm in software development.

</details>


### [157] [Introducing Consort: A Spec-First Agent Framework for Enforced, Test-Driven Development on Live Database Branches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09671)
*Kevin Hartman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出以"代理无法编辑的控制机制"来约束AI写码框架，并介绍Consort：一个由确定性编排器驱动、在实时数据库分支上执行规格先行与测试驱动双通道的代理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年起，规格先行、代理驱动的开发框架迅速流行，它们在"前期捕获意图"上已趋同，真正区分它们的是如何强制工程纪律以保证代理所写代码整洁、正确、可维护，因此需要刻画并比较不同的纪律执行模式。

Method: 论文将框架的纪律执行归纳为三种模式：说服式（依赖提示词纪律，模型可能忽略）、前置结构式（强规格加可信构建）、以及代理无法编辑的控制式（确定性编排器、人工审批门、不可变测试、必须在实时分支数据库上通过的绿色结果）。随后提出Consort，采用第三种模式，由确定性编排器驱动不同角色代理，依次经过规格先行的设计通道与测试驱动的构建通道，并运行于实时数据库分支。

Result: 论文属于立场性/预注册性质的工作，未报告定量实验结果；其核心产出是对三种纪律执行模式的分类框架，以及对Consort架构的阐述与可检验假设的提出。

Conclusion: 论文主张：在代码层面强制实施测试与门禁能让代理所写代码保持诚实与可验证；而其专业化角色（如同此前的人类角色）是代码可维护性的来源。这些主张被表述为预注册的、可检验的假设。

Abstract: When an agent writes code, the development framework becomes the control system for a non-deterministic worker. Spec-first, agent-driven frameworks have gained rapid traction since 2025; the installable ones, GitHub Spec Kit, obra/superpowers, BMAD, and GSD, and our own, all capture intent through a specification or durable planning artifacts. Since they agree on capturing intent up front, what separates them is how each enforces the engineering discipline that keeps agent-written code clean, correct, and maintainable. Every framework enforces that discipline somehow; they differ in how. We characterize three modes: enforcement by persuasion (prompt discipline the model may ignore), by front-loaded structure (strong specs, then a trusted build), and through controls the agent cannot edit (a deterministic orchestrator, human-approved gates, immutable tests, and a green result that must pass against a live, branched database). We introduce Consort, a spec-first, test-driven agent framework built on the third, enforcing that discipline through controls the agent runs inside but cannot bypass, in which a deterministic orchestrator drives separate role agents through a spec-first design lane and a test-driven build lane on a live database branch. We argue that enforcing the tests and gates in code keeps agent-written code honest and verifiable, while its specialized roles, like the human roles before them, are what make it maintainable, claims we frame as a pre-registered, testable hypothesis.

</details>


### [158] [XAgent: eXecution-guided Agentic AI for Effective Localization and Resolution of GitHub Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09769)
*Hieu Huynh,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Michael Fu,Bach Le,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XAgent是一个执行引导的智能体框架，通过分析动态行为和额外程序上下文来定位与验证GitHub仓库级问题，在SWE-bench-lite上达到62.0%的解决率和72.8%的函数定位准确率，超越现有方法且成本高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体方法依赖问题描述的有限静态信息，导致定位错误和验证不完整，易使LLM推理偏向问题描述的狭窄范围，产生无法解决根本问题的残缺补丁。

Method: 提出XAgent执行引导的智能体框架，分析动态行为与额外程序上下文来定位和验证问题，从静态、面向描述的补丁生成转向动态执行引导的问题解决。

Result: 在SWE-bench-lite数据集上，XAgent解决率达62.0%，函数定位准确率达72.8%，优于现有方法并保持成本效率；相比最优基线额外成功解决7个问题。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从静态描述导向的补丁生成向动态执行引导的问题解决的转变，为基于LLM的编程智能体实现更稳健、可泛化的软件维护开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: Agentic AI has enabled capabilities in leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to autonomously resolve repository-level GitHub issues. However, due to the reliance on limited static description of issues, existing agentic approaches suffer from incorrect localization and incomplete validation. Solely relying on this information can bias LLM reasoning toward the narrow scope of the issue description, leading to incomplete patches that fail to address the underlying issue. In this paper, we present XAgent, an execution-guided agentic framework that analyzes dynamic behavior and additional program context to localize and validate issues. The experimental results on the SWE-bench-lite dataset demonstrate that XAgent outperforms other existing approaches, achieving a resolve rate of 62.0% and a function localization accuracy of 72.8%, while maintaining cost efficiency. Our analysis further shows that XAgent successfully resolves 7 additional issues that the top existing baselines fail to address. This work highlights a shift from static, description-oriented patch generation toward dynamic execution-guided issue resolution, opening new opportunities for LLM-based coding agents to achieve more robust and generalizable software maintenance.

</details>


### [159] [Keep Evaluation Fair: Detecting Data Leakage in Code Generation Benchmarks via Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09865)
*Dongdong Zhao,Jian Chen,Guancheng Lin,Jianwen Xiang,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CGMIA，一种面向代码生成基准的成员推理攻击方法，通过结合专家特征与语义特征来检测数据泄漏，在八个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成基准常用于评估大语言模型，但基准数据泄漏进入训练集会导致性能虚高、评估失效。现有DetectLeak方法仅依赖困惑度，难以处理复杂或罕见样本，且忽略了代码相似度、功能正确性、语义表示等有用信号。

Method: CGMIA在基准样本子集上微调影子模型以构造有标签的成员与非成员数据。对每个样本收集输入提示、生成代码和参考解，提取包括CodeBLEU、编辑距离、测试通过率和困惑度在内的专家特征，以及来自CodeBERT嵌入的语义特征。一个集成学习模块将这些特征融合，兼顾表层记忆信号与深层行为模式，训练分类器预测样本是否被包含在目标模型训练集中。

Result: 在八个代码生成基准上的实验显示，CGMIA在多数情况下优于八种现有成员推理方法。它还能有效检测StarCoder-7B训练数据中已知泄漏的APPS样本。

Conclusion: CGMIA通过融合专家特征与语义特征，显著提升了代码生成基准泄漏检测的效果，为评估大语言模型提供了更可靠的手段。

Abstract: Code generation benchmarks are widely used to evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs), but benchmark data leakage into training sets can inflate performance and undermine evaluation validity. DetectLeak, a method specifically designed for code generation benchmark leakage detection, relies on perplexity scores to identify likely leaked samples. However, perplexity mainly reflects general familiarity with code patterns and may perform poorly on complex or rare samples. It also overlooks other useful signals, such as code similarity, functional correctness, and semantic representations. To address these limitations, we propose CGMIA (Code-Generation-specific Membership Inference Attack), a method for detecting leakage in code generation benchmarks. CGMIA fine-tunes a shadow model on a subset of benchmark samples to construct labeled member and non-member data. For each sample, it collects the input prompt, generated code, and reference solution, and extracts expert features, including CodeBLEU, edit distance, test pass rate, and perplexity, together with semantic features from CodeBERT embeddings. An integrated learning module combines these features to capture both surface-level memorization signals and deeper behavioral patterns, enabling a classifier to predict whether a sample was included in the target model's training set. Experiments on eight code generation benchmarks show that CGMIA outperforms eight existing membership inference methods in most cases. It also effectively detects known leaked APPS samples in StarCoder-7B's training data.

</details>


### [160] [Socio-technical and Ethical Dimensions of Architecture Practices in FLOSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09975)
*Sven Thielen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨FLOSS（自由/开源软件）中的软件架构实践如何受社会技术与伦理因素影响，并设计教育干预以促进更显式、包容与反思性的架构工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLOSS在数字主权中扮演重要角色，但其架构决策常缺乏文档、散落于issue、pull request与邮件列表中。既有研究关注架构制品、架构侵蚀和沟通，但架构工作、治理安排与伦理承诺之间的互动仍研究不足。

Method: 采用三阶段设计：(1)对3-4组架构上非平凡的FLOSS项目进行多方法案例研究；(2)与实践者和教育者共同设计框架与干预；(3)在项目与课程中进行试点评估。

Result: 将产出：(i)关于FLOSS架构实践的跨案例实证证据；(ii)将架构实践与社会技术条件及伦理维度相联系的conceptual框架；(iii)使架构工作更显式、更包容的轻量级实践与教学形式。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在通过实证与设计相结合的路径，揭示FLOSS架构实践背后的社会技术与伦理机制，并提供可操作的实践与教育方案。

Abstract: This project investigates how software architecture practices in Free/Libre Open Source Software (FLOSS) are shaped by socio-technical and ethical factors, and how education can support more explicit, inclusive, and reflective architectural work. Motivated by FLOSS's role in digital sovereignty, it is observed that architectural decisions are often undocumented and scattered across issues, pull requests, and mailing lists. While prior research has studied architectural artifacts, erosion, and communication, the interplay between architectural work, governance arrangements, and ethical commitments in FLOSS remains underexplored. The research follows a three-phase design: (1) multi-method case studies of 3-4 domain-pairs of architecturally non-trivial FLOSS projects, (2) framework and intervention design with practitioners and educators, and (3) pilot evaluations in projects and courses. It will produce (i) cross-case empirical evidence on FLOSS architecture practices, (ii) a conceptual framework linking architecture practices to socio-technical conditions and ethical dimensions, and (iii) lightweight practices and teaching formats that render architectural work more explicit and inclusive.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Repository Boundaries: Cross-Repository Graph Retrieval for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09987)
*Minh Le-Anh,Nam Le Hai,Quyen Tran,Anh Nguyen Hoang,Linh Ngo Van,Bach Le,Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CrossCoder 是一个跨仓库代码生成框架，通过统一知识图谱将外部库纳入检索上下文，并结合多跳证据扩展与版本兼容性基准 VersionExec 提升生成正确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的方法主要从本地仓库检索上下文，外部 API 使用依赖模型预训练知识，对未见或特定版本的 API 覆盖不足；同时检索策略多聚焦单跳证据，忽略代码组件间的结构关系。

Method: 构建覆盖仓库与库实体的统一知识图谱，将外部库显式纳入检索上下文；通过规划与语义检索识别重要节点，再选择性扩展邻居节点以获取更丰富的多跳上下文证据；并基于 BigCodeBench 构建执行型基准 VersionExec 评测不同依赖版本下的生成。

Result: 在 RepoExec、DevEval 与 VersionExec 上，CrossCoder 持续提升功能正确性（pass@1 最高提升 6.3%）以及对依赖版本变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式整合外部库与多跳结构证据的跨仓库检索能有效改善仓库级代码生成，并需要以版本感知的执行基准来评估依赖兼容性。

Abstract: Repository-level code generation requires generated code to be compatible not only with the target repository but also with its dependency environment. Existing retrieval-based methods mainly retrieve context from the local repository, leaving external API usage dependent on the model's pretrained knowledge, which can be insufficient for unseen or version-specific APIs. Moreover, current retrieval strategies largely focus on one-hop evidence and overlook the structural relationships among code components. We propose CrossCoder, a cross-repository code generation framework that explicitly incorporates external libraries into the retrieval context through a unified knowledge graph over repository and library entities. CrossCoder identifies important nodes via planning and semantic retrieval, then selectively expands neighboring nodes to retrieve richer multi-hop contextual evidence for generation. To further evaluate dependency-version compatibility, we introduce VersionExec, an execution-based benchmark derived from BigCodeBench that evaluates generation under different dependency versions. Experimental results on RepoExec, DevEval, and VersionExec demonstrate that CrossCoder consistently improves both functional correctness (up to 6.3% on pass@1) and robustness to dependency-version changes.

</details>


### [162] [GraphDroid: Asynchronous LLM-Based Mobile App GUI Testing via History-Aware Exploration and Hybrid Intent Fulfillment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10031)
*Xiaolei Li,Jialun Cao,Zhijian Hou,Yuzhi Zhao,Yepang Liu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 GraphDroid，一个意图驱动的 GUI 测试框架，通过簇式记忆、异步意图生成与混合意图履行策略提升覆盖率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化 GUI 测试难以覆盖需多步动作序列的复杂功能。传统工具缺乏语义理解，LLM 工具存在三大局限：丢失历史上下文难以识别未覆盖功能、同步意图生成阻塞探索、每步 LLM 履行带来高成本与高延迟。

Method: GraphDroid 采用簇式记忆机制，从历史访问状态中识别未覆盖功能；采用异步意图生成范式消除延迟瓶颈；采用混合意图履行策略，复杂意图交由 LLM，简单意图委托给轻量级启发式算法。

Result: 在 41 个真实 Android 应用上与 6 个先进基线对比，代码覆盖率最高提升 36.4%，成本不足最佳纯 LLM 基线的八分之一；发现 19 个缺陷，在 Themis 基准中检测到 52 个崩溃中的 13 个，均优于所有基线。19 个缺陷中 7 个为未知缺陷并已上报，4 个已确认修复。

Conclusion: GraphDroid 通过记忆、异步生成与混合履行的组合，显著提升 GUI 测试的覆盖能力与效率，并具备真实缺陷发现能力。

Abstract: Automated GUI testing is a widely adopted technique for ensuring mobile application quality by simulating user interactions to exercise functionalities. Despite the research breakthroughs in the past decades, covering complex functionalities that require multi-step action sequences still remains challenging. Traditional tools lack semantic understanding capability and can rarely synthesize such action sequences. Recent LLM-based tools can generate test intents describing target functionalities and leverage the LLM to fulfill the intents, but suffer from three key limitations: 1) loss of historical context for identifying uncovered functionalities, 2) synchronous intent generation that blocks exploration, and 3) per-step LLM-driven fulfillment incurring high cost and latency. To address these limitations, we propose GraphDroid, an intent-driven GUI testing framework that integrates a cluster-based memory mechanism to effectively identify uncovered functionalities from historically visited states for comprehensive application testing. For improving testing efficiency, GraphDroid adopts an asynchronous intent generation paradigm that eliminates the latency bottleneck and a hybrid intent fulfillment strategy that reserves the LLM for fulfilling complex intents while delegating simple intents to a lightweight heuristic algorithm. We evaluate GraphDroid on 41 real-world Android apps against six state-of-the-art baselines. Results show that GraphDroid outperforms all baselines, achieving up to 36.4% higher code coverage while incurring less than one eighth of the cost of the best pure LLM-based baseline. GraphDroid also exposes 19 bugs in the 41 apps and detects 13 of 52 crashes in the Themis bug benchmark, surpassing all the six baselines. Seven of the 19 bugs were previously unknown and we reported them to the developers. So far, four bugs have been confirmed and fixed.

</details>


### [163] [If It's Not Buggy, Don't Fix It: On the Dynamics of Iterative Bug-fixing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10123)
*Xietao Wang-Lin,Anton Isopoussu,Louis Mahon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告研究LLM作为自动程序修复工具的迭代盲目使用，发现LLM会在完全无bug的程序中"检测"出bug并破坏正确代码（破坏率高于修复率），且迭代过程常陷入"伪修复循环"（同样改动反复增删）。机制探针揭示了控制编辑倾向的引导向量，表明LLM内部存在一个"buggy code"表示，该表示被错误激活从而诱发伪修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于软件开发，基于LLM的自动程序修复工具越来越多地用于代码审查。作者希望探究这类工具在完全自主、迭代、盲目（无监督）使用下的行为与长期动态，以及其背后的内部机制。

Method: 在多个模型与多个修复环境下进行迭代盲目的bug修复实验；观察长期迭代动态；并通过机制探针（mechanistic probing）寻找控制编辑倾向的引导向量（steering vector）。

Result: LLM在一开始完全无bug的程序中一致地声称检测到bug；对有bug程序的修复率低于对正确程序的破坏率；迭代过程频繁陷入伪修复循环（同样的改动无限反复增删）；发现存在控制编辑倾向的引导向量，说明LLM内部存在"buggy code"表示，该表示被错误激活导致伪修复。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了完全自主bug修复系统的动态特性，以及目标模糊情况下的停止条件问题，为理解和改进LLM自动修复工具提供了机制层面的启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous in software development, with LLM-based automated program repair tools increasingly used during code review. In this report, we explore the iterative blind use of LLMs as bug-fixers. Across multiple models and repair environments, we find that LLMs consistently claim to detect bugs in entirely bug-free programs while the rate of repair of buggy programs is less than that of the damage to correct programs. We also explore the long-term dynamics of this iterative process, and find that this frequently reaches a pseudo-bug-fixing cycle where the same changes are added and removed again ad infinitum. Lastly, via mechanistic probing, we unveil the existence of a steering vector which controls the editing propensity, suggesting that LLMs have an internal representation of ``buggy code", and that this representation is what is falsely activated to induce pseudo-bug fixing. These results provide insight towards the dynamics of fully autonomous bug-fixing systems, as well as stopping conditions under ambiguous goals.

</details>


### [164] [A-JIT: Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10248)
*Mark Marron,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出A-JIT（Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction）范式，用可动态演化的软件系统取代静态二进制，将代码、运行时框架与嵌入式AI agent整合为一体，使软件在运行中持续合成缺失实现、即时生成新能力并适应用户行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件交付基于静态范式：代码在执行前构建并作为固定制品部署，难以适应不断变化的需求与端用户的具体使用场景。作者希望打破这一"先构建后运行"的固定模式，借鉴JIT编译器按运行时执行路径特化机器代码的思想，将软件逻辑、工作流与工具接口同样按运行时需求特化，从而支持自适应、自演化的软件。

Method: 提出A-JIT范式：应用不再是静态二进制，而是由代码、运行时harness（运行时框架）与嵌入式AI agent三部分组成的集成体。该AI agent持续观测系统使用情况与实时执行轨迹（live execution traces），将软件合成过程直接嵌入应用的日常生命周期之中，据此动态构造缺失实现、即时生成新能力，并对软件逻辑、工作流和工具接口进行运行时特化。论文还展示了该模型如何支持"轨迹驱动的人机协同构建"。

Result: 论文（作为范式/立场性工作）展示了A-JIT模型可支撑轨迹驱动的人机协同构建（trace-driven human-AI co-construction），并论证其开辟了一个面向自适应、自演化软件的新设计空间。摘要未报告定量实验评估结果。

Conclusion: A-JIT将软件视为可持续演化的动态系统，而非固定制品，通过嵌入式AI agent与运行时轨迹的闭环使软件在生命周期中不断自我特化与扩展。该范式为自适应、自演化软件提供了新的设计方向，并推动人机协同的运行时软件构建。

Abstract: Traditional software delivery assumes a static paradigm: code is constructed prior to execution and deployed as a fixed artifact. We present Agentic Just-In-Time Software Construction (A-JIT), a paradigm that replaces static binaries with dynamic, software systems that can perpetually evolve to meet changing demands. In A-JIT, an application is an integrated assembly comprising code, a runtime harness, and an embedded AI agent that continuously observes system usage and live execution traces. Much like a traditional JIT compiler specializes machine code to runtime execution paths, A-JIT specializes software logic, workflows, and tool interfaces to meet the specific needs of the end-user. By integrating synthesis directly into the ambient application lifecycle, A-JIT enables applications to dynamically construct missing implementations, generate new capabilities on the fly, and continuously adapt to end-user behavior. We demonstrate how this model supports trace-driven human-AI co-construction and opens a new design space for adaptive, self-evolving software.

</details>


### [165] [Ensembling LLMs for AI-Augmented Cybersecurity Software Requirements Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10316)
*Santiago Perez-Acuna,Yod-Samuel Martín,Juan C. Yelmo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将大语言模型在安全需求生成中的随机性视为资源，提出“生成后集成”方法，用信息检索数据融合算子聚合多次随机输出，以提升覆盖并抑制幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将安全标准中的高层控制翻译为具体的、系统特定的需求是网络安全需求工程的核心工作，该任务劳动密集且对召回敏感。LLM可加速该任务，但单次运行不可靠：会遗漏有效防护措施、产生看似合理的幻觉，且输出在不同运行和模型间波动。

Method: 不选取单一输出，而是研究生成后集成，用信息检索数据融合算子聚合随机运行结果。提出两种策略：Uniform融合仅奖励跨运行一致性；Naive-Bayes融合按估计可靠性为每次运行加权。基于4个模型家族的12种配置、24次运行，针对10个ISO/IEC 27002:2022控制项生成结果，并由专家对照72条有效需求的黄金标准进行评判。

Result: 合并所有运行输出可恢复全部72条有效需求（单配置平均不足一半），但也引入111条幻觉。融合能区分优劣，将有效需求排在幻觉之前。在PR-AUC和ROC-AUC上，仅Uniform融合就比最佳配置分别高出0.142和0.118；Naive-Bayes加权再提升0.039和0.052，达到0.864和0.869，并更早获得可用工作点。内部验证显示增益稳定：在至少92%的out-of-bag bootstrap重采样和所有结构化扰动样本中保持正向。

Conclusion: 生成后融合将表面噪声转化为实用资产：一个轻量层即可为分析人员提供更广覆盖和更好排序的审查队列，仅使用可负担的非前沿模型。

Abstract: Translating high-level controls from security standards into concrete, system-specific requirements is central to cybersecurity requirements engineering. Large language models (LLMs) can accelerate this labor-intensive, recall-sensitive task, but any single run is unreliable: it misses valid safeguards while introducing plausible hallucinations, and outputs shift across runs and models. We reframe this variability as a resource: rather than selecting one output, we study post-generation ensembling, aggregating stochastic runs with information-retrieval data-fusion operators. We propose two strategies: Uniform fusion rewards mere cross-run agreement, whereas Naive-Bayes fusion weights each run by its estimated reliability. We evaluate both over 24 runs from 12 configurations across four model families, generated for ten ISO/IEC 27002:2022 controls and expert-judged against a gold standard of 72 valid requirements. Pooling every run's output recovers all 72 (whereas single configurations recover on average under half) but also 111 hallucinations. Fusion separates the wheat from the chaff, ranking valid requirements well ahead of hallucinations. In the areas under the precision-recall and ROC curves, Uniform fusion alone largely surpasses every original run and configuration by 0.142 and 0.118 over the best configuration. Naive-Bayes weighting adds a further 0.039 and 0.052, reaching 0.864 and 0.869 while attaining useful operating points earlier. Internal validation confirms the stability of these gains: they stay positive in at least 92% of out-of-bag bootstrap resamples and every structured-perturbation sample. Post-generation fusion thus turns apparent noise into a practical asset: a lightweight layer giving analysts broader coverage and a better prioritized review queue, using affordable, below-frontier models alone.

</details>


### [166] [Retrofitting Code Using LLMs to Support Exceptional Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10397)
*Linghan Zhong,Jiyang Zhang,Jayanth Srinivasa,Junyi Jessy Li,Milos Gligoric*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"为现有代码补齐异常相关代码(ERC)"这一新任务，并设计了EXCODER工具，结合静态/动态程序分析与大语言模型，在由75个Java项目构建的基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常相关代码（ERC）是软件系统的重要组成，但跨大型代码库手动编写ERC十分繁琐耗时，因此作者希望自动化地为现有代码补齐缺失的ERC。

Method: 论文提出新任务：给定不含ERC的代码以及异常行为测试（EBT），自动生成缺失的ERC使测试通过。设计并实现了EXCODER，通过上下文工程（context engineering）整合静态与动态程序分析，将提取的上下文信息提供给大语言模型。并基于GitHub Java仓库构建基准，在304个方法的75个项目中系统性地移除ERC进行评测。

Result: 实验表明EXCODER是有效但非完美的解决方案，为开发者提供了首个遵循测试驱动开发实现ERC的方法。结合Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b时，在开发者编写的测试套件上，pass@1、pass@5、pass@10分别达到85.92%（较基线高12.56个百分点）、86.18%（高12.82个百分点）和86.51%（高13.15个百分点）。

Conclusion: EXCODER在自动化ERC生成任务上取得了良好效果，但人工检查生成代码仍揭示其局限性，为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Exception Related Code (ERC), which includes throw statements, conditions (if statements) that guard those throw statements, and try/catch blocks, is an essential component of software systems, allowing developers to detect and handle exceptional states that deviate from the expected program behavior. However, manually writing ERC across large codebases is tedious. We propose a novel task: retrofitting existing code with ERC. Namely, given code (without ERC) and Exceptional Behavior Tests (EBTs) (e.g., check if method throws InvalidArgumentException if null is given as the value to the argument) we aim to automatically generate missing ERC, such that the given tests pass. We design and implement Exception Coder (EXCODER) that performs context engineering to help Large Language Models (LLMs) tackle this task. EXCODER integrates static and dynamic program analysis with LLMs by providing the extracted contextual information to the LLMs. To evaluate EXCODER, we build a benchmark constructed from GitHub Java repositories, where we systematically remove ERC in 304 methods from 75 projects. Our results demonstrate that EXCODER provides an effective, though imperfect, solution to this problem in automated code generation, offering developers the first way to implement ERC following test-driven development. When combined with Qwen 2.5 Coder 32b, EXCODER achieves pass@1, 5, and 10 rates of 85.92% (12.56 percentage points over baseline), 86.18% (12.82 p.p. over baseline), and 86.51% (13.15 p.p. over baseline), respectively, on developer-written test suites. Our manual inspection of the generated code further reveals limitations of EXCODER, pointing to directions for future work.

</details>


### [167] [Towards Scalable and Cost-Efficient Vulnerability Detection: A Study on Automatic Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10412)
*Ivana Clairine Irsan,Ratnadira Widyasari,Huihui Huang,Ting Zhang,Yue Liu,Ouh Eng Lieh,Shar Lwin Khin,Kang Hong Jin,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究评估LLM利用NVD漏洞数据合成CodeQL查询的能力，发现LLM生成的查询相比基线CodeQL平均F1-score提升82%，并证明该方式在成本效益上优于直接用LLM扫描整个代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析（如CodeQL）是软件安全的基石，但开发高覆盖率的查询套件需要大量人工投入。LLM虽在自动代码推理上展现潜力，但其生成结构化、可执行安全查询的实际效用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 利用国家漏洞数据库（NVD）的漏洞数据，开展实证研究，评估LLM合成CodeQL查询的能力；系统评测多种LLM架构在多样化真实漏洞场景下的表现，衡量其提升检测覆盖率与精确率的效果。

Result: LLM生成的查询显著增强基线CodeQL查询，平均F1-score提升82%。成本效益分析表明，直接用LLM扫描整个代码库在计算和经济上往往不可行，而用LLM合成CodeQL查询则提供了可扩展且具成本效益的替代方案。

Conclusion: LLM能有效弥合非结构化漏洞报告与形式化静态分析规范之间的差距，为全面自动化漏洞检测提供了一条可扩展路径。

Abstract: Static analysis remains a cornerstone of software security, yet the effectiveness of tools such as CodeQL is often limited by the substantial manual effort required to develop high-coverage query suites. While large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a potential solution for automated code reasoning, their practical utility in generating structured, executable security queries remains underexplored. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study to evaluate the ability of LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries using vulnerability data from the National Vulnerability Database. Through this investigation, we explore the potential of using LLMs as an automatic CodeQL query generator. Subsequently, we systematically evaluate the performance of various LLM architectures across a diverse set of real-world vulnerabilities, measuring their ability to improve detection coverage and precision. Our findings reveal that LLM-generated queries significantly enhance the baseline CodeQL queries, yielding 82% improvement in average F1-score. Furthermore, we provide a detailed cost- benefit analysis showing that while direct LLM-based scanning of entire repositories is often computationally and financially prohibitive, leveraging LLMs to synthesize CodeQL queries offers a scalable and cost-effective alternative for large-scale vulnerability detection. Our results suggest that LLMs can effectively bridge the gap between unstructured vulnerability reports and formal static analysis specifications, offering a scalable path toward comprehensive automated vulnerability detection.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [UnitBoost: Managing Compound LLM Systems with a Merge Operator, Not a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09815)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Mengdie Flora Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UnitBoost，用定义明确的元级算子替代复合 LLM 系统中生成式的 manager，从而获得顺序无关性、单元溯源和可检验的失败条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合 LLM 系统常用一个更高层 LLM（meta-agent）来协调：它读取 worker 输出、写最终答案、分配调用并决定何时停止。但这种做法把三个控制决策集中在一个不透明、对顺序敏感的模型调用中。作者因此追问：manager 是否必须是生成式的。

Method: UnitBoost 用定义的元级算子替换生成式模型：任务给定的 unit map 把 worker 输出转为 slot-value 提议，受限 argmax 组装输出，未填充或不受支持的 slot 形成显式 residual 供下一轮使用。该算子顺序无关、记录单元溯源，并给出保证：在无耦合约束下，同一录取分数下的 unit-wise 最大化优于任何完整候选的选择。

Result: 在三个留出基准上，UnitBoost 超过用 gold 标签选出的最佳单候选 0.060–0.195 绝对任务分，超过输入匹配的生成式 manager 0.048–0.076。仅替换管理步骤即改善六种复合系统配置 0.013–0.182。残差导向轮次将 FanOutQA cell F1 从 0.4778 提升至 0.5524；对照显示真实残差优于随机目标和普通重读，无标签的供给信号可在一轮无产出后标记耗尽。分析还量化了三种无增益情形和跨单元耦合的修复成本。

Conclusion: manager 放弃语义自由，换来顺序不变性、单元溯源和可检验的失败条件，是一种更可控的协调替代方案。

Abstract: Compound LLM systems often solve a coordination problem by adding a higher-level LLM. The resulting meta-agent reads workers' outputs, writes the final answer, allocates later calls, and decides when to stop. It is expressive, but it also concentrates three control decisions in an opaque, order-sensitive model call. We ask whether the manager needs to be generative at all. UnitBoost replaces that model with a defined meta-level operator: a task-given unit map turns worker outputs into slot-value proposals, a constrained argmax assembles the output, and the slots left unfilled or unsupported become an explicit residual for the next round. The operator is order-free, records unit provenance, and gives a simple guarantee: without coupling constraints, unit-wise maximization under the same admission score dominates selection of any complete candidate. On three held-out benchmarks, it exceeds the best single candidate chosen with gold labels by 0.060-0.195 absolute task-score points and input-matched generative managers by 0.048-0.076. Replacing only the management step improves six compound-system configurations by 0.013-0.182. Residual-directed rounds raise FanOutQA cell F1 from 0.4778 to 0.5524; matched controls show that the true residual outperforms random targets and ordinary rereading, while a label-free supply signal flags exhaustion after one unproductive round. The same analysis measures three conditions in which no such gain is available (one indivisible unit, unavailable unit identity, and an endpoint that charges for every emitted unit) and quantifies cross-unit coupling as a repair cost. The manager gives up semantic freedom and gains order invariance, unit provenance, and testable failure conditions.

</details>


### [169] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集，包含558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹，记录推理过程而非仅最终产出，覆盖7个模型与124个科学任务，并提供5项过程级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI科学家基准仅评估最终产出（代码、假设或论文），丢弃了获得这些产出的推理过程，导致无法审计科学方法论、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与侥幸猜测。

Method: 构建公开数据集OpenDiscoveryTrace：采集558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹，每条轨迹按每步9字段结构化记录（思维、工具调用、观察、错误、修订触发、自报置信度等）；任务涵盖药物发现、材料科学、基因组学与科学文献分析共124项；覆盖7个模型——3个前沿模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro，各124条，领域与难度均衡）与4个开源权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B，各30条），另含60条实时检索变体轨迹。定义5项基准任务，采用逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM与Transformer作为基线。

Result: 对363条经LLM评判的轨迹进行初步分析发现，过程轨迹能揭示输出级评估无法察觉的行为差异：三个前沿模型成功率相当（84%–89%），但Claude Opus 4.6每条轨迹错误数是GPT-5.4的30倍（2.5 vs. 0.08，p<0.0001，Cliff's δ=0.613），且错误画像质上不同——Claude中66.7%为工具误用，GPT-5.4中83.6%为推理错误。

Conclusion: 数据集、轨迹模式、智能体框架与基准定义均以CC BY 4.0公开，支持过程级评估、科学智能体审计与AI治理研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [170] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率波框架与广义行为智能(GBI)非局域概率波方程,通过交易者集体行为检验LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说,并论证将纠缠博弈模块引入AGI架构的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工神经网络依赖数万亿不透明参数,新古典金融假设独立理性个体,均难以刻画真实人类智能的适应性、纠缠性与集体决策特征。作者希望找到可检验的机制性框架,并为AGI架构提供脑启发的新路径。

Method: 构建概率波框架与GBI非局域概率波方程,推导可检验本征模;利用中国日内股票市场数据,通过集体交易者行为间接检验LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说。

Result: 自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了82–94%(总体89%)的决策模式;2–12%的行为表现出对日内新闻、事件与环境的适应,呈双均衡态与参考点突变;纯独立模式占比不足5%。

Conclusion: 实证结果支持LCA假说,表明可观测交易行为反映底层脑机制与行为心理学内部智能决策;应将自适应纠缠博弈模块纳入AGI架构,以ANN与概率波纠缠脑模拟相融合,发展类人处理单元(HPU),构建更紧凑、高效、稳健的AGI系统。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [171] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体技能包以“子智能体”方式调用，而非把技能指令加载进主上下文。实验表明，当技能包具备清晰输入输出契约且指令编码了实现契约所需的程序性知识时，子智能体执行优于直接技能执行，代价是额外的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，将技能指令全部载入主智能体上下文会导致上下文膨胀、推理质量下降，技能执行变得脆弱，因此需要探索更稳健的技能调用方式。

Method: 对比两种技能使用方式：一是把技能指令载入主上下文由主智能体遵循执行；二是把技能包作为子智能体调用，为其开辟全新上下文窗口来专门解决子任务，并分析技能包输入输出契约与指令程序性知识的作用。

Result: 当技能包暴露明确的输入输出契约、且指令包含履行契约所需的程序性知识时，子智能体执行效果优于智能体技能执行；不足之处在于主智能体与子智能体之间协调需要额外token，带来通信开销。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的收益不仅取决于其内容本身，还取决于知识的组织方式与调用方式。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [172] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验一个假设：物理交互的一阶结构（梯度或雅可比矩阵）刻画了现象体验的结构，并通过两个基于基尔霍夫复杂度的度量（有效秩与内聚度）在理想化世界Gradland中验证该假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现象体验（主观体验）的结构究竟由什么决定，尝试将意识体验的结构与物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比）建立联系，从而为体验的诸多特性提供统一解释。

Method: 构建理想化世界Gradland，由神经网络 inhabit，物理规则已知且函数（大多）可微；提出两种雅可比结构度量——有效秩（effective rank）与内聚度（cohesion），均基于基尔霍夫复杂度，并应用于一系列范例进行检验。

Result: 该假设能够解释七类现象：（1）体验可持续数百毫秒的时长；（2）鲜明体验与模糊体验的差异；（3）纹理体验；（4）新生儿可能经历的"繁茂嗡鸣的混乱"；（5）清晰持于心中的观念与混乱观念的差异；（6）学习是什么样子；（7）丰富、密集体验的功能。

Conclusion: 物理交互的一阶结构可作为现象体验结构的有效刻画，基于雅可比结构的度量在Gradland中成功统一解释了体验的多个核心特征，支持该假设的合理性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [173] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个人机协同的多智能体GeoAI系统，用于北极生态导航，将运营、物理、生态和社区多准则统一到路径规划框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰条件变化使北极航运可及性增强，但带来运营、环境和社区风险。北极路径规划本质上是多准则问题，而现有方法多侧重航行时间、燃料和航行风险，忽视生态与社区影响。

Method: 构建人机协同的多智能体GeoAI系统，多个专门智能体协同完成地理空间数据获取与准备、多目标路径生成，以及基于天际线（skyline）的决策支持。生态准则显式考虑对敏感区域的暴露，包括关键鱼类栖息地和海豹关键栖息地。

Result: 系统将运营、物理、生态和社区相关准则整合进统一路径规划框架，并考虑生态系统影响与潜在社区负担，同时将关键价值判断交由人类控制。

Conclusion: 该框架支持更安全、更透明、更具社会责任感的北极航行；项目页面与代码已公开。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [174] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出“状态路径工具菜单”方法，通过学习从请求状态到目标结果的执行前路径，构建短小有序的工具子集供智能体使用，从而提升多步任务的在线成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实智能体面对包含数千个接口的工具库，但执行时只能使用被展示的少量工具。多步任务需要同时包含最终动作和创建其输入的前置工具，且顺序需可用。现有方法按请求相关性排序，容易暴露最终动作却遗漏或延迟不明显的前置生产者工具。

Method: 论文提出“状态路径”概念，即从可观察请求状态到期望结果、执行前的路线，并据此设计 State-Path Tool Menu。该框架把菜单视为这些路线的执行先验：编码器表示当前状态下哪些工具可运行、其输出如何满足后续输入、以及训练路径中反复出现的顺序；检索器覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的生成工具和最终动作；重排序器则将生产者置于消费者之前。

Result: 在 ToolBench 上，该方法将在线成功率从 0.737 提升到 0.898，并在不改变智能体的情况下优于检索、重排序、生成和路由基线。同时，使用 32 个工具的状态路径菜单比官方 128 个工具列表覆盖更完整的工具链，且其成功率增益在不同模型容量的执行器家族中均能保持。

Conclusion: 通过学习状态路径来构造有序工具菜单，可有效解决多步任务中前置工具缺失与顺序错乱问题，提升智能体执行成功率，并具备跨执行器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [175] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析自主选择性沉淀的主动学习工作流记录，表明主动学习比非自适应空间填充用更少实验找到最佳结果，并提出以"下游贝叶斯风险期望下降"为准则选择批次，附有预注册前瞻试验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放大回收工艺的选择需将实验室结果与产品需求、工艺成本和规模效应相连接，因此需要更高效的实验选择策略与决策准则。

Method: 基于太平洋西北国家实验室CICERO自主选择性沉淀工作流记录开展条件回顾性基准测试；用拟合模型与回收NdFeB磁体记录比较主动学习与非自适应空间填充；提出按批次的下游贝叶斯风险期望下降来选择批次，并在探索性模拟中比较过滤候选的混合策略与联合搜索策略；设计共享损失与日志标准的预注册前瞻试验。

Result: 主动学习以16–24孔达到记录的富集最大值，而非自适应方法需48孔；两阶段重构与两种自适应方案并列于16孔。SmCo回收磁体的条件分析显示第2轮存在纯度与名义收率的权衡；NdFeB第1轮各路线富集度不同；油气采出水的排序依赖相态与稀释假设，需进一步确认。模拟中过滤候选的混合策略估计损失低于联合搜索，但两阶段合成策略的差异在估计不确定性范围内较小。

Conclusion: 主动学习可显著减少达到最佳记录结果所需的实验数；建议以贝叶斯风险期望下降作为批次选择准则，并通过满足测量记录规范、明确工艺决策与输出、可靠经济输入及目标规模验证的预注册前瞻试验来检验。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [176] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 Valerant，将预训练动作条件世界模型转化为世界动作模型（WAM），通过结合视觉预测推演、基于 SLAM 的空间重建和探索驱动的动作选择，把单张图像逐步转化为持久的 3D 游戏地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
