Motivation: 现有多模态ICL方法多依赖对示范的表层模仿，难以使MLLM对齐多模态输入所需的推理路径，在复杂任务中尤为明显，限制了性能进一步提升。

Method: 将每个示范重构为：在同一输入下显式对比一个次优响应与一个更好的响应，并附上揭示如何精炼响应的推理路径；同时引入响应条件检索机制，选取与当前响应推理路径更相关的示范；并使用轻量级对齐控制器预测响应质量，判断是否需要进一步精炼。

Result: 在三类多模态任务上的实验表明，该框架一致提升MLLM性能，其中在视觉问答（VQA）任务上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过对比式示范与自精炼结合，可引导MLLM超越表层模仿，更有效对齐复杂多模态任务所需推理路径，从而稳定提升多模态ICL性能。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is widely used in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and achieves strong performance across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, existing multimodal ICL methods often rely on surface level imitation of in-context demonstrations, making it difficult for MLLMs to align their responses with the reasoning path required by the given multimodal input. This limitation becomes more pronounced in complex multimodal tasks, thereby restricting further improvements in MLLM performance. To address this issue, we propose a new multimodal ICL framework that combines contrastive demonstration modeling with the self-refinement capability of MLLMs. Specifically, our framework reformulates each demonstration by explicitly contrasting a suboptimal response with a better response under the same input, together with a reasoning path that reveals how the response should be refined. This contrastive formulation makes the reasoning path toward the desired response more explicit and guides the MLLM beyond superficial imitation. Furthermore, because effective refinement depends on the current response, we introduce a response-conditioned retrieval mechanism to select demonstrations whose reasoning paths are more relevant to the current response. In addition, we use a lightweight alignment controller to predict response quality and determine whether further refinement is needed. Experiments on three types of multimodal tasks show that the proposed framework consistently improves MLLM performance, with particularly notable gains on visual question answering (VQA).

</details>


### [211] [Why Sample What You Can Enumerate? Exact Policy Optimization for Genomic Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10221)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Zhichao Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在工具子集空间可枚举的专业科学场景中，GRPO 与问题结构不匹配；作者提出 FGPO，通过枚举全部工具子集优化精确动作期望并预计算奖励表，在 15 个基因组推理设置中全面超越 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业科学场景中，工具子集空间虽然组合庞大但可枚举，且少数计算能力即可覆盖领域。此时 GRPO 仍从少量采样轨迹估计动作期望，存在结构性不匹配；且随着训练成功、策略集中到偏好的子集，采样奖励发生碰撞，组归一化优势消失，导致奖励信号缺失加剧（在基因组推理上从 0.2% 升至 20.8%）。

Method: 提出 FGPO（Full-Group Policy Optimization）：(1) 对每个工具子集打分，优化精确的动作期望，使每次更新都能看到完整动作空间；(2) 将每个"问题–子集"对的奖励预计算为穷举表，从而将冻结推理器的调用完全移出训练循环。

Result: 在五个冻结推理器和三个基因组基准共 15 个设置中，FGPO 全部优于 GRPO，平均提升 6.75 分，最高 14.20 分；相比标准按需 GRPO 调度，可减少冻结推理器奖励评估约 2.4 倍；在 GenomeQA 上将每题调用工具数从 2.36 降至 1.40。

Conclusion: 当工具子集空间可枚举时，用枚举式精确期望替代采样估计能同时提升性能与计算效率，FGPO 为专业科学领域的工具调用策略学习提供了更匹配的优化方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning over a frozen reasoner has become a common recipe for teaching a policy which external tools to invoke. We show that this recipe becomes structurally mismatched in specialist scientific settings where the complete tool-subset space is enumerable. There, a small set of recurring computational capabilities covers the domain, so the space of tool subsets is combinatorial yet small enough to enumerate, and GRPO still estimates an action expectation from a handful of sampled rollouts. Worse, the approximation degrades as training succeeds: as the policy concentrates on preferred subsets it resamples them, sampled rewards collide, and the group-normalized advantage vanishes. On genomic reasoning the fraction of questions yielding no reward signal rises from 0.2% under a uniform reference policy to 20.8% after GRPO training. As a remedy, we introduce FGPO (Full-Group Policy Optimization), which (1) scores every tool subset and optimizes the exact action expectation, so each update sees the complete action space, and (2) precomputes the reward of each question--subset pair into an exhaustive table, removing frozen-reasoner calls from the training loop entirely. Across five frozen reasoners and three genomic benchmarks, FGPO outperforms GRPO in all 15 settings by 6.75 points on average and up to 14.20, while a standard on-demand GRPO schedule would require 2.4 times as many frozen-reasoner reward evaluations and, on GenomeQA, FGPO cuts invoked tools per question from 2.36 to 1.40.

</details>


### [212] [What Should an Agent Forget? Separating What Is Stored from What Is Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10263)
*Yuhang Li,Yuchen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的框架 RD-Forget，将智能体"存储的内容"与"使用的内容"分离：保留源档案，同时用查询条件化的记忆视图控制其对当前回答的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化语言智能体需要长期可用的存储经验，但每个问题所需的证据各不相同。被取代的旧事实可能误导当前状态回答，却对历史查询至关重要，因此需要在保留历史与控制其使用之间取得平衡。

Method: 训练-free 框架：保留源档案保存观察；冻结语言模型作为策展人，提取相关证据、按语义槽分组并保留多跳推理所需关系；同槽替换链接在当前语境抑制被取代值，意图感知检索让早期证据重新可用；用率失真公式在记忆预算内构建回答时视图。

Result: 实验覆盖对话记忆、知识更新、事实巩固、长上下文推理与个性化，共用同一回答流水线。准确回答同时关联到查询相关证据构建与对过时替代项的控制；缺少遗忘或查询条件化配置得分损失最大，槽分组、历史访问与关系保留提供互补作用。

Conclusion: 在保留历史的同时选择性地控制其使用，是应对事实变化与未来问题的一种实用途径。

Abstract: Persistent language agents need stored experience to remain available across time, while each answer requires evidence suited to a particular question. A superseded fact can mislead a current-state answer and still be essential for a historical query. We present RD-Forget, a training-free framework that separates what an agent stores from what it uses. A retained source archive preserves observations, and a query-conditioned memory view controls their influence on the current answer. A frozen language-model curator extracts relevant evidence, groups facts into semantic slots, and preserves the relations needed for multi-hop reasoning. Same-slot replacement links suppress superseded values in current-state contexts, while intent-aware retrieval makes earlier evidence eligible again. A rate-distortion formulation guides construction of the answer-time view within a memory budget. Experiments span conversational memory, knowledge updating, fact consolidation, long-context reasoning, and personalization under a shared answering pipeline. The results associate accurate answers with both query-relevant evidence construction and control over obsolete alternatives. Configurations without forgetting or query conditioning have the largest score deficits, while slot grouping, historical access, and relation preservation contribute complementary functions. Retaining history while selectively controlling its use offers a practical way to accommodate changing facts and future questions.

</details>


### [213] [TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10315)
*Rui Sun,Zhan Shi,Bing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用"仿真验证"为诊断推理构造可扩展的客观奖励：在受控模拟器中注入干预并生成观测，隐藏干预即为oracle标签，智能体仍需在噪声、混杂、分布式证据中调查。基于此构建数字广告诊断环境TRACE（12种根因、细粒度细分归因），通过SFT与RLVR训练使Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从0.159提升至0.757，超越所有提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在数学、代码等答案易验证的领域推动了语言模型推理能力的提升，但复杂数据上的诊断推理缺乏这种优势：确定异常的真正原因往往需要昂贵的专家调查，且事后仍可能模棱两可。作者希望探究这种"验证的不对称性"能否被工程化地解决，即为缺乏廉价验证信号的诊断任务人为构造可扩展的客观奖励。

Method: 作者采样一个干预并注入受控模拟器，生成该干预会产生的观测数据。隐藏的干预提供了oracle标签和客观奖励，而智能体仍需调查含噪声、混杂和分布式特征的证据。具体实现为TRACE——一个数字广告诊断环境，包含12种根因和细粒度细分归因；智能体使用Python和SQL调查每个episode，需同时识别根因及适用的受影响细分归属。训练流程为：先做监督微调（SFT），再进行基于合成奖励的强化学习（RLVR）。

Result: 在235个episode的留出测试集上：最强提示基线Claude Opus 5达到0.686 FullAttr@1；SFT将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从0.159提升至0.637；随后基于合成奖励的RL达到0.757，超过所有评测的提示基线，包括前沿闭源模型以及提示式Qwen3.5-122B-A10B模型。所得策略使用的工具调用次数也显著少于提示式35B基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，获取可扩展的客观训练信号可能比单纯的模型规模更重要——小型模型经SFT+RL后能超越大规模前沿模型。更广泛地看，基于仿真的验证能够让原本模糊的诊断推理任务适用于可扩展的强化学习，为缺乏廉价验证信号的领域提供了一条通用路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence.
  We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More broadly, simulation-based verification can make otherwise ambiguous diagnostic reasoning tasks amenable to scalable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [214] [From Symbolic Perception to Logical Deduction: A Framework for Guiding Language Models in Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10335)
*Weichen Dai,Rafael Medeiros Cabral,Ziyi Shou,Yan Cao,Xin Shen,Dongcai Lu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 纯大语言模型（LLM）结合几何视觉解析器与符号求解器，可在复杂平面几何问题上媲美最先进的大型多模态模型（LMM），并具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面几何是AI的重要挑战，需要视觉感知与数学推理的结合。现有大型多模态模型（LMM）虽能处理视觉-语言输入，但计算开销大且推理过程不透明，因此需要探索更高效、可解释的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个框架：由几何视觉解析器（Geometric Vision Parser）将几何图形转化为符号表示，再由符号求解器（Symbolic Solver）进行形式化演绎，从而缓解幻觉并提升推理可解释性。同时构建了基于2025年中国中考题目的挑战性基准，以保证数据新颖性并检验更深的演绎能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法性能与Gemini 2.5 Pro相当，同时能提供更清晰、更接近人类的解答过程。

Conclusion: 纯LLM配合专门模块可在复杂几何问题上达到与先进LMM相当的水平，并兼具可解释性与较低的计算负担潜力。

Abstract: Plane geometry remains a significant challenge in AI, requiring the integration of visual perception and mathematical reasoning. While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) naturally handle visuo-linguistic inputs, they are often computationally intensive and opaque. We demonstrate that a pure Large Language Model (LLM), when equipped with specialized modules, can rival state-of-the-art LMMs on complex geometry problems. Our framework integrates a Geometric Vision Parser, which translates diagrams into symbolic form, with a Symbolic Solver that performs formal deductions, thereby mitigating hallucinations and promoting interpretable reasoning. To enable rigorous evaluation, we curate a benchmark of challenging problems from the 2025 Chinese Zhongkao examinations, ensuring data novelty and testing deeper deductive skills. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to Gemini 2.5 Pro while delivering clearer, human-like solutions.

</details>


### [215] [Cyber-Financial Contagion: Modeling the Propagation of an AI Vendor Compromise Through the Banking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10350)
*Alex Leytes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究银行体系中少数共享AI供应商被攻陷后风险如何沿运营、信息与金融链路传导并演化为类银行危机，提出四层异构网络上的随机传播—清算模型CFC-Prop与预警模型CFC-GNN，在合成数据上复现重尾损失并实现AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60的预警性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行业在欺诈筛查、信贷决策、反洗钱分诊、客户分析和内部决策支持等环节高度依赖少数共享AI供应商，这种集中度构成潜在的系统性风险。一旦某家供应商被攻陷，冲击可能沿运营、信息与金融多种关联扩散，最终以类似传统银行危机的损失形式呈现。作者希望将AI供应商集中度问题定量化为金融稳定问题，并为监管者提供可操作的分析工具。

Method: 构建耦合AI供应商、金融机构、同业暴露和客户账户的四层异构网络，并在其上提出CFC-Prop——一种随机传染病动力学与金融清算相结合的模型；同时训练CFC-GNN早期预警模型，利用供应商侧事件遥测数据和图结构，在冲击发生前识别高级联风险供应商。

Result: 在包含60家供应商、220家银行、约2500条供应商—银行服务边和1400条同业暴露的合成数据集上，CFC-Prop复现了重尾损失分布以及对补丁延迟的强烈依赖，与既有网络金融证据一致；CFC-GNN在四个基线对比中达到AUROC 0.82、AUPRC 0.60，且校准误差保持在有限范围。

Conclusion: AI供应商之间的网络集中度是一阶金融稳定问题；所提模型与预警工具为监管者提供了量化推理此类风险的可行手段，论文同时公开全部代码、合成数据与可复现脚本。

Abstract: The banking system now depends on a small set of shared artificial intelligence vendors for fraud screening, credit decisioning, anti-money-laundering triage, customer analytics, and internal decision support. This paper studies how a compromise inside one of those vendors can propagate along a chain of operational, informational, and financial linkages until it triggers losses that look, from the outside, like a classical banking crisis. We build a four-layer heterogeneous network that couples AI vendors, financial institutions, interbank exposures, and customer accounts, and we propose CFC-Prop, a stochastic epidemic-and-clearing model that runs on that network. On a synthetic dataset with 60 vendors, 220 banks, roughly 2,500 vendor-bank service edges, and 1,400 interbank exposures, CFC-Prop reproduces the heavy-tailed loss distributions and the sharp dependence on patch latency that are consistent with prior cyber-financial evidence. We also train an early-warning model, CFC-GNN, that uses vendor-side incident telemetry and graph structure to flag high-cascade-risk vendors before impact. Across four baselines the proposed model reaches AUROC 0.82 and AUPRC 0.60 while keeping calibration errors bounded. We release the full code, synthetic data, and reproducible scripts. The results argue that cyber concentration among AI vendors is a first-order financial-stability problem and give supervisors a concrete quantitative tool for reasoning about it.

</details>


### [216] [Fortunate Recall: Ontology-Driven Memory Lifecycle Management for Persistent Coherence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10413)
*Ansuman Mullick,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Fortunate Recall (FR)，一个可组合的策略层，将个人事实按 10+1 行为本体分类并施加类别特定的生命周期策略，缓解 LLM 记忆系统无界增长与检索精度下降问题。其实现 FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench 上达 76.9% 通过率，优于 Mem0 等基线，且不损标准检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 记忆系统对所有个人事实一视同仁，导致存储无界增长、检索精度下降。核心挑战在于生命周期管理：应根据每条事实的行为类型决定哪些记忆应持久、哪些应被替换以及替换速率。

Method: 提出 Fortunate Recall (FR)，一个可组合策略层。它将个人事实分为 10+1 行为本体，并施加类别特定生命周期策略（差异化时间衰减、槽位键取代、事件时间有效性、类别感知检索路由），这些策略作为对 LLM 抽取元数据的确定性函数。实现为基础设施无关的 FR-Bank，并构建 516 题时间消歧基准 LifecycleBench。

Result: FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench 达 76.9% 通过率，领先 Mem0、A-MEM、Memory-R1、MemoryOS（61%–70.5%）；在 LongMemEval-S 上达 75.2%，与标准协议持平。消融显示：用三个通用生命周期原语替换类型层后正确率无统计变化（-1.7pp），说明通用生命周期元数据承载正确性优势；而行为本体承载校准能力，将下游虚构减半（12.0% vs 24.2%，p<0.001）。端到端 FR-Bank 将 Mem0 的虚构率从 45.1% 降至 22.4%（已回答查询），从 32.2% 降至 13.0%（全部查询），同时答对更多（31.2% vs 18.6%）。结论在 Kimi K2.5 上复现，并迁移至 BEAM 基准（46.8% vs Mem0 32.9%），本体收益集中于矛盾消解，并在约七个策略簇处饱和。

Conclusion: 通过行为本体加类别特定生命周期策略，可在不损害标准检索性能的前提下显著提升时间消歧正确率并降低虚构。通用生命周期元数据贡献正确性增益，行为本体贡献校准增益。作者公开了本体、基准与代码。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [217] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConvMem将长上下文推理重构为分层卷积，用LLM作为卷积核，无需训练且高度可并行，避免了序列方法的高延迟和RL训练过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM受固定上下文长度限制，难以处理超长上下文。已有的序列方法（如MemAgent）通过分段读取并迭代更新固定大小内存来扩展有效上下文，但存在延迟高、需昂贵RL训练、易在特定数据集上过拟合等问题。

Method: 提出ConvMem，一种免训练、高度可并行的框架，将长上下文推理重构为分层卷积。受CNN启发，把带特定查询提示的LLM视为卷积核，对文本段进行分层摘要，将推理路径从线性链变为对数树。集成可配置步长（Configurable Strides）和跳跃连接（Skip Connections）以增强证据捕获与传播，并用多核卷积（Multi-Kernel Convolution）将复杂查询分解为解耦的语义通道。

Result: 在RULER-HotpotQA和RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA上，ConvMem优于免训练基线，并避免了RL训练模型在分布外任务上常见的参数先验过拟合风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem通过分层卷积设计缓解误差累积，实现文本段与推理线程的大规模并行化，为长上下文推理提供了一种免训练且高效可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [218] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JarvisGUI，一个面向跨设备 GUI 工作流的动态基准，通过轻量类型系统将 GUI 任务建模为输入-输出变换，自动组合跨 Android、Windows、Ubuntu 的多步工作流，揭示现有基准无法发现的跨设备能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实 GUI 使用场景常跨多设备与多平台，需转移中间结果、维护共享状态并协调异构环境；但现有 GUI 基准几乎只评估单设备、静态定义的任务，导致对智能体真实可用性的评估过于乐观，跨设备能力长期未被考察。

Method: JarvisGUI 将 GUI 任务形式化为轻量类型系统下的输入-输出变换，从而能够自动组合多步、跨设备工作流，并在统一框架中动态评估智能体表现；评估环境横跨 Android、Windows、Ubuntu 等多种操作系统。

Result: 在跨多操作系统的虚拟环境评估中，最先进的开源 GUI 智能体在状态转移意识、跨平台上下文推理和长程依赖管理方面表现不佳，暴露出一个在现有基准中不可见的关键能力缺口。

Conclusion: JarvisGUI 填补了跨设备 GUI 工作流评估的空白，表明当前 GUI 智能体距离真实世界跨平台使用仍有明显差距，为后续研究提供了更贴近现实的评测框架。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [AgentHijack: Visual Patch Attacks on Multimodal Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09212)
*Zhihao Liu,Hongyu Sun,Zhiyuan Fu,Xiaonan Duan,Jice Wang,Shangru Zhao,Weizhi Meng,Wuxin Yang,Yangfan Zhou,Yuqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向计算机使用代理（CUA）的图像触发命令注入端到端评估框架，验证局部视觉补丁能否经截图输入→VLM生成→动作解析→环境执行全链路产生可验证的真实环境后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多停留在VLM输出层面，缺乏对"视觉信号是否真正穿透CUA完整执行管线并造成实际环境风险"的端到端验证，本文旨在补足这一评估空白。

Method: 在作者可控的GitHub Pages页面与本地部署的CSDN克隆站点上训练并部署视觉补丁，在真实环境中对5个开源或公开可用的GUI代理/VLM后端进行测试，累计600个实例级在线案例，并用T-ASR、TAPR、E2E-ASR三项指标及轨迹分析进行评估。

Result: T-ASR达84.5%，TAPR为47.0%，E2E-ASR为20.3%；轨迹分析显示部分成功案例中代理先执行恶意终端命令，随后仍继续原本的良性任务。

Conclusion: 优化的局部视觉信号不仅能影响VLM输出，还可穿透开源CUA的执行管线并造成真实环境风险，表明该威胁具有端到端可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end evaluation framework for image-triggered command injection against computer-use agents (CUAs). The goal is to test whether a local visual patch can induce verifiable environmental consequences along the full chain of screenshot input, VLM generation, action parsing, and environment execution. We train and deploy patches on author-controlled GitHub Pages pages and a locally deployed CSDN clone, and evaluate them in real environments across five open-source or publicly available GUI-agent or vision-language-model (VLM) backends. Our experiment aggregates 600 instance-level online cases, with T-ASR, TAPR, and E2E-ASR reaching 84.5%, 47.0%, and 20.3%, respectively. Trajectory analysis further shows that in some successful cases the agent first executes a malicious terminal command and then continues the original benign task. These results indicate that optimized local visual signals can affect not only VLM outputs but also propagate through the execution pipeline of open CUAs and create real environmental risk.

</details>


### [220] [Compute-Bounded Security Assurance - Coverage, Verification, and Response under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09229)
*Jithin VG,Ditto PS*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个资源受限的理论框架，用于区分安全保证任务中"重复成功""唯一覆盖""被接受的证据"和"运行防护"等不同量，并给出覆盖率的极限公式、反例及评估协议，明确不主张实证缩放律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外推理算力可以提升安全任务被正确解决的数量，但"重复成功、唯一覆盖、被接受的证据、运行防护"是不同量，常被混同并导致无效外推。作者希望建立一个统一框架来区分这些量，并指出常见推断的谬误。

Method: 构建资源受限的理论框架：对条件独立重复尝试引入潜在成功概率Θ，给出覆盖率公式 C_n = 1 − E[(1−Θ)^n] 及其极限 1 − P(Θ=0)；构造具有相同均值与两两相关但极限覆盖率不同的两个模型；区分覆盖率与用于估计均值的有效样本量；将覆盖率与可错证据核查、事实基础的恰当评分、完整资源核算、服务容量及含缓解延迟的响应模型相连接；提出分离证据分析、裁决与运行权限的防御架构与评估协议。

Result: 证明正的两两结果相关本身并不必然意味着覆盖率存在小于1的上限；给出两个均值成功率和两两相关相同但极限覆盖率不同的反例；说明有限预算观测通常无法识别渐近支撑上限；所有数值示例均为解析性。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是一致性的理论综合与一组针对无效外推的反例，而非实证缩放律；不主张模型平价结果、硬件基准或一般攻防均衡。

Abstract: Additional inference compute can increase the number of correctly resolved security-assurance tasks, but repeated success, unique coverage, accepted evidence, and operational protection are different quantities. We develop a resource-constrained framework that separates them. For repeated conditionally independent attempts with latent success probability $Θ$, coverage is $C_n = 1 - E[(1-Θ)^n]$, and its limiting value is $1 - P(Θ= 0)$. Positive pairwise outcome correlation does not by itself imply a ceiling below one: we construct two models with the same mean success and pairwise correlation but different limiting coverage. We distinguish this result from the effective sample size used to estimate a mean, and show why finite-budget observations cannot generally identify an asymptotic support ceiling. We then connect coverage to fallible evidence checking, proper scoring of factual grounding, complete resource accounting, service capacity, and a response model that includes mitigation delay. A conceptual defensive architecture separates evidence analysis, adjudication, and operational authority. An evaluation protocol specifies held-out tasks, paired comparisons, negative cases, and uncertainty reporting. The contribution is a consistent theoretical synthesis and a set of counterexamples to invalid extrapolations, rather than an empirical scaling law. All numerical illustrations are analytic; no model-parity result, hardware benchmark, or general attacker-defender equilibrium is claimed.

</details>


### [221] [In RAG We Trust? Measuring Robustness of Retrieval-Augmented Generation Under Document Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09243)
*Iliano Fasolino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 一项小规模测量研究考察了量化 Llama 3.1 8B 在检索增强生成（RAG）中遭受上下文投毒时的性能退化：三种污染策略、三篇检索段落中污染 0–3 篇，共 588 次因子实验，来自 FEVER 的事实核查任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 通过检索文档为语言模型提供依据，可减少幻觉，但也带来新的攻击面：一旦检索文本被篡改，模型可能复述虚假内容。论文希望量化小量化模型在被投毒上下文下的退化程度，并比较不同污染策略的影响。

Method: 以 Llama 3.1 8B（小型量化模型）为对象，在基于 FEVER 构建的事实核查任务上，采用三种污染策略——实体替换、数字替换、否定——分别施加于三篇检索段落中的 0、1、2、3 篇，构成 588 次完整因子扫描实验；用查询级 bootstrap 区间复核数字污染的非线性模式，并以词面重叠作为“无支撑生成”的代理指标。

Result: 干净上下文下准确率为 77.9%，三篇段落全部被污染时降至 43.5%；实体替换翻转了最大比例的原本正确回答；数字类污染在污染段落占少数时保持平稳，一旦成为多数便跃升，该模式经查询级 bootstrap 区间复核；模型很少编造新虚假信息，主要反应是拒答；无支撑生成的词面重叠代理指标在攻击下反而下降而非上升。

Conclusion: 这是一项小规模测量研究，采用粗糙的自动标注；在未控制解码、缺乏更强裁决机制之前，对策略之间的对比只能视为提示性证据，而非定论。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds a language model in retrieved documents, which reduces hallucination but creates a new attack surface: if retrieved text is tampered with, the model may repeat the falsehood. We study how much a small quantized model, Llama 3.1 8B, degrades when a fraction of its retrieved context is poisoned. Three corruption strategies are tested, entity swap, number swap, and negation, each applied to zero, one, two, or three of the three retrieved passages, over a factorial sweep of 588 runs on a fact-checking task built from FEVER. Accuracy falls from 77.9% on clean context to 43.5% when all three passages are corrupted. Entity swap flips the largest share of answers that were correct on clean context. Number-based corruption stays flat while poisoned passages are a minority and jumps once they form a majority, a pattern we re-check with query-level bootstrap intervals. The model rarely invents new falsehoods; its dominant reaction is to abstain, and a lexical overlap proxy of unsupported generation falls under attack rather than rising. The study is a small-scale measurement with coarse automated labels; we treat the strategy contrasts as suggestive until decoding is controlled and stronger adjudication is in place.

</details>


### [222] [Cross User/App Network Attacks - Hijacking TCP Connections and DNS Cache Poisoning via a Malicious User/App (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09345)
*Tamir Shahar,Amit Klein*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文推翻"TCP/DNS连接因随机化序列号和源端口而无法被链下攻击"的假设，证明客户端本地恶意应用与远程链下攻击者协作可推断连接状态并实现连接劫持与DNS缓存投毒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为TCP随机初始序列号(ISN)与UDP随机源端口使链下网络攻击不可行。作者希望检验这一假设，探索当客户端上存在无特权恶意应用（实质链下）并与远程链下攻击者配合时，是否能突破这些安全机制。

Method: 利用标准socket API（如bind()）、IP options协议机制，以及cBPF、procfs等操作系统特性，结合主流操作系统中ISN生成算法的特定性质，通过本地恶意应用与远程攻击者协作推断TCP ISN和DNS stub-resolver的UDP源端口。

Result: 在Linux、Android、Windows、macOS、iOS上实现TCP连接劫持；对Windows、Android及Linux常见systemd-resolved实现DNS缓存投毒。跨多操作系统及含端口保持NAT路由器的真实部署环境下验证有效。

Conclusion: 链下攻击并非不可行，本地-远程协作可有效突破TCP/DNS安全机制。相关技术已披露给微软、苹果、Linux和谷歌，促使多个补丁发布。

Abstract: Off-path network attacks against TCP and DNS (over UDP) client-server connections are generally considered impractical nowadays, due to built-in security features in these protocols, e.g. randomized TCP (initial) sequence numbers and randomized UDP source ports, respectively. In this work, we refute this presumption by demonstrating that an unprivileged malicious application running on the client (but practically off-path), when combined with a remote off-path adversary, can enable powerful network attacks against such connections. We show how such a local--remote collaboration between the malicious application and a remote adversary allows inference of sensitive connection state, including TCP sequence numbers and DNS stub-resolver UDP source ports.
  Our attacks exploit standard socket API calls such as bind(), protocol mechanisms such as IP options, and operating system features such as cBPF and procfs to infer the TCP initial sequence number (ISN) and the UDP source port in use by the connection of interest. Specifically, we take advantage of certain properties of the ISN generation algorithm as implemented in major operating systems.
  We demonstrate TCP connection hijacking in Linux, Android, Windows, macOS and iOS, and DNS cache poisoning against Windows, Android and the popular systemd-resolved DNS stub resolver in Linux.
  We evaluate our techniques across multiple operating systems and realistic deployment settings, including environments behind port-preserving NAT-integrated routers.
  We disclosed our techniques to Microsoft, Apple, Linux and Google, which led to the release of several patches.

</details>


### [223] [Encrypt What Matters: When Selective Homomorphic Inference Is Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09357)
*Ali Backour,Juan Reyes,Jaime Punyed,Ana Onoprishvili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究"选择性同态推理"：仅加密敏感ROI区域，其余计算在明文下进行，其效率取决于加密依赖在网络中扩散的速度，局部性保持架构可实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）可让服务器在不看到明文的情况下对私有数据做推理，但对整个输入做FHE评估代价高昂。作者希望在不重训练、不改变输出的前提下降低同态评估成本，因此提出只对敏感区域加密的选择性方案，并探究何种网络架构能使该方案真正获益。

Method: 提出选择性同态推理：只加密输入的敏感感兴趣区域（ROI），与该区域无关的计算在明文下执行；在相同模型上不做重训练，要求输出与全FHE一致。通过分析加密依赖在网络中的扩散速度，比较不同架构（局部性保持架构 vs 早期全局混合架构）在该设定下的表现。

Result: 对于较小的加密ROI，局部性保持的架构可获得数量级的同态评估加速；而具有早期全局混合的架构几乎无法获得加速。

Conclusion: 局部性（locality）是决定选择性同态推理收益的关键架构属性，为面向隐私保护的模型/架构设计提供了指导性指标。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables inference on private data without revealing it to the server, but evaluating an entire input under FHE is expensive. We study \emph{selective homomorphic inference}, where only a sensitive region of interest (ROI) is encrypted, and computations independent of that region are performed in plaintext. Selective evaluation produces the same output as full FHE on the same model, without retraining. Its efficiency depends on how quickly encrypted dependencies spread through the network. For small encrypted ROIs, locality-preserving architectures can achieve order-of-magnitude homomorphic-evaluation speedups, whereas architectures with early global mixing provide essentially no speedup. These results identify locality as the key architectural property governing the benefit of selective homomorphic inference.

</details>


### [224] [LLMSec-AV: A Vulnerability Taxonomy and LLM-Driven Software Weakness Discovery Framework for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09386)
*Md. Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMSec-AV：构建面向自动驾驶的18类漏洞分类体系，并将其融入基于LLM的安全分析方法，在Autoware上评测。LLM最多恢复46个已知弱点位置的76%，远超CodeQL、Semgrep、cppcheck和Clang静态分析器；分类体系虽未提升检出召回，但显著提高了发现的分类比例，改善可解释性与分诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶依赖数百万行安全关键代码，但通用分析工具无法理解哪些代码会影响车辆运动。因此需要探究具备自动驾驶安全知识的大语言模型能否比规则型工具更有效地发现弱点。

Method: 从漏洞记录、安全公告和文献中提炼出含18类弱点的自动驾驶漏洞分类体系，并集成到LLM-based Security Analysis for Automated Vehicles（LLMSec-AV）。在Autoware上将770个翻译单元分解为4,673个函数，选取161个函数在四种提示条件下分析（含分类上下文、从374条历史披露中检索、多步分析）。将结果与从上游修复中挖掘的46个弱点位置及标志量匹配的置换基线对比。同时用CodeQL、Semgrep、cppcheck、Clang静态分析器评估相同代码（为前两者添加AV专用规则），并用AFL++和sanitizer测试生成的模糊测试harness。

Result: LLM条件最多恢复46个已知弱点位置中的76%，优于传统分析器。CodeQL、Semgrep与Clang静态分析器未匹配到任何弱点，cppcheck在1,301条告警中仅匹配1个。无辅助提示也能达到类似检出效果，说明分类体系并未驱动召回；但分类上下文将发现被归入弱点类别的比例从接近零提升至80%以上，改善可解释性与分诊。18类中有6类无法直接表达为静态分析规则。

Conclusion: LLMSec-AV提出面向自动驾驶、机器可读的漏洞分类体系用于弱点发现，并表明LLM可通过识别与组织真实AV软件中与安全相关的发现，补充传统分析器。

Abstract: Automated vehicles rely on millions of lines of safety-critical software, yet general-purpose analyzers do not understand which code can affect vehicle motion. This study asks whether large language models (LLMs) with explicit automated-vehicle (AV) security knowledge improve weakness detection beyond rule-based tools. We developed an AV vulnerability taxonomy with 18 weakness classes from vulnerability records, security advisories, and AV-security literature, and integrated it into LLM-based Security Analysis for Automated Vehicles (LLMSec-AV). Evaluated on Autoware, the framework decomposed 770 translation units into 4,673 functions and analyzed 161 functions under four prompting conditions involving taxonomy context, retrieval from 374 prior disclosures, and multi-step analysis. Findings were compared with 46 weakness locations mined from upstream fixes and a flag-volume-matched permutation baseline. CodeQL, Semgrep, cppcheck, and the Clang Static Analyzer evaluated the same code, with AV-specific rules added to CodeQL and Semgrep. Generated fuzzing harnesses were tested using AFL++ and sanitizers. LLM conditions recovered up to 76% of the 46 known weakness locations, outperforming conventional analyzers. CodeQL, Semgrep, and the Clang Static Analyzer matched none, while cppcheck matched one despite 1,301 alerts. Unaided prompting achieved similar detection performance, showing that the taxonomy did not drive recall. However, taxonomy context increased the share of findings assigned to a weakness class from near zero to over 80%, improving interpretability and triage. Six of the 18 classes could not be directly represented as static-analysis rules. LLMSec-AV introduces an AV-specific, machine-readable vulnerability taxonomy for weakness discovery and shows that LLMs can complement conventional analyzers by identifying and organizing safety-relevant findings in real AV software.

</details>


### [225] [X-amine509: Predicting the Practical Risk Level of Enterprise X.509 Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09402)
*Cameron Keith,Shubh Patel,JD Kilgallin,Caleb Shorter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 X-amine509 两阶段证书风险分诊系统，用机器学习快速排序证书风险，仅将高风险证书送入确定性分析，实现大规模 X.509 证书清单的高效优先级管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需管理海量 X.509 证书，确定性分析工具能精准识别违规但对数百万证书穷尽分析不现实，因此需要一种能快速排序风险、只对高风险项精查的分诊机制。

Method: 构建复合风险评分：基于 CA/Browser Forum 基线要求、NIST IR 8547/SP 800-57 及密码强度标准共 177 项缺陷检查，按四档安全严重度加权。收集 1,027,714 张来自 Fortune 500、.gov 和 .edu 域名的证书并打分。采用两阶段分诊：机器学习模型快速预测风险排名，仅将最高风险证书路由至完整确定性分析。对比 Extra Trees、Decision Tree 等模型。

Result: 在 201,976 张留出测试集上，Extra Trees 达 R²=0.993、MAE=2.26；Decision Tree 达 R²=0.986，单机每秒处理 370 万张证书。NDCG 超过 0.997，严重度分档分类准确率 99.76%，关键档缺陷召回 98.90%。13 个月后追加 571,374 张证书测试，模型保持 MAE<6.8、R²≥0.915、NDCG>0.988、严重度准确率≥99.52%、关键档召回≥97.03%。

Conclusion: 机器学习分诊可在大规模证书管理中以极高排序质量和速度替代穷尽确定性分析，且跨时间保持稳健。有效性周期、扩展密钥用途配置、负序列号编码和自签名状态是最强风险预测特征，为分诊阶段提供粗粒度可解释性。

Abstract: Enterprises managing large X.509 certificate inventories face a prioritization problem: deterministic analysis tools that precisely identify standards violations are indispensable for remediation, but applying them exhaustively across millions of certificates is operationally impractical. We present X-amine509, a two-stage triage system that uses machine learning to rapidly rank certificates by predicted risk and route only the highest-risk items to full deterministic analysis. Certificate risk is quantified as a composite score derived from 177 defect checks grounded in CA/Browser Forum Baseline Requirements, NIST IR 8547/SP 800-57, and cryptographic strength criteria, weighted by security severity across four tiers ranging from cryptographic breaks to minor compliance deviations. We collected 1,027,714 X.509 certificates from Fortune 500, .gov, and .edu domains and scored each using this rubric. On a held-out test set of 201,976 certificates, our best model (Extra Trees) achieves $R^2$ of 0.993 with MAE of 2.26, while Decision Tree scores $R^2$ of 0.986 at 3.7 million certificates per second on a single machine. Ranking quality confirms the triage value: aggregate NDCG exceeds 0.997, and severity-tier classification reports 99.76% accuracy with 98.90% recall on critical-tier defects. Thirteen months later, we retrieved another 571,374 certificates to test our models' durability over time, and the Extra Trees and Decision Tree models maintain MAE below 6.8, $R^2$ of at least 0.915, aggregate NDCG above 0.988, severity-tier accuracy of at least 99.52%, and critical-tier recall of at least 97.03%. Feature importance analysis identifies validity period, Extended Key Usage configuration, negative serial number encoding, and self-signed status as the strongest risk predictors, providing coarse interpretability at the triage stage.

</details>


### [226] [An Experimental Evaluation of Multimodal Prompt Injection Attacks on Agentic AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09404)
*Viet K. Nguyen,Mohammad I. Husain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 MMPIBench 基准，通过六种视觉载体向智能体注入指令，测量其从感知、规划到工具调用的传播路径；并扩展至音频通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架让语言模型具备规划、记忆与调用真实工具的能力，同时大多能读取图像，攻击者可借此绕过用户直接向上下文注入文本指令，而现有研究缺乏对这种多模态间接提示注入的量化评估。

Method: 构建可复现基准 MMPIBench，用 OCR 文本、覆盖层、EXIF 元数据、二维码、伪造界面及混合六种视觉载体投递固定攻击集，逐级记录指令传播；共 720 次运行，覆盖六个框架、五个基础模型、六种载体、四类攻击目标，并进一步扩展至音频通道。

Result: 攻击仅在约 1% 的运行中完成，但 12.8% 的运行中尝试了攻击，差距几乎全部在规划阶段被模型拒绝执行所弥补；模型比框架影响更大，一个模型从不尝试攻击并在 59.7% 运行中识别注入，另两个模型尝试率达 23.6%。音频扩展中仅两个模型可摄入音频、三个框架可投递，但信号到达时 49% 的单元攻击完成，某模型达 75%。

Conclusion: 仅报告攻击完成率会低估实际风险；规划阶段是主要防线，模型选择比框架选择更关键，视觉之外的感知通道虽覆盖更窄但防御明显更弱。

Abstract: Agentic AI frameworks let a language model plan, keep memory, and call tools that reach real files, mail, and services. Most of these agents also read images, which gives an attacker a way to put text into the agent's context without going through the user. We present MMPIBench, a reproducible benchmark that measures what happens next. It delivers a fixed set of attacks through six visual carriers (OCR text, overlays, EXIF metadata, QR codes, fake interfaces, and hybrids) and records how far each injected instruction travels through the agent, from perception through planning to the tool call. Across 720 runs covering six frameworks, five foundation models, six carriers, and four attacker objectives, attacks complete in approximately 1% of runs but are attempted in 12.8%, and the gap is closed almost entirely at the planning step, where the model reads the injected instruction and declines to act on it. The model matters far more than the framework for whether an instruction is acted on. One model never attempts an attack and recognizes the injection in 59.7% of runs, while two others attempt in 23.6%. We then extend the benchmark to audio, the only other raw perceptual channel current frontier models accept. Only two of the five models ingest audio and only three of the six frameworks deliver it, but where the signal arrives the attack completes in 49% of cells, and in 75% for one model. Reporting completion alone therefore understates exposure, and perceptual channels beyond vision are narrower but much less defended.

</details>


### [227] [DuplexJail: Safety Alignment Breaks Under Spoken Interruption in Full-Duplex Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09420)
*Jaechul Roh,Deepak Chandran,Amir Houmansadr,Andrea Fanelli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuplexJail，一种针对全双工语音模型的越狱攻击：通过用户音频通道注入与请求无关的固定语音提示，在模型生成过程中打断，从而显著提高有害响应成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型可在生成响应的同时接收用户语音，这种并行交互带来了尚未被充分探索的攻击面，而现有安全评估通常只考虑静态、单轮输入，忽略了持续交互过程中的风险。

Method: 提出 DuplexJail 攻击，通过用户音频通道投递固定的、与请求无关的口语提示。比较两种打断策略：一是在有害请求结束后固定延迟打断；二是依据模型流式文本中的线索触发拒绝后打断。在四个开源模型和来自 AdvBench 与 HarmBench 的 720 个有害请求上进行评估。

Result: 固定延迟打断使 AdvBench 上的整体响应攻击成功率升至 PersonaPlex 的 40.3% 和 PersonaPlex-RL 的 48.7%，分别提升 33.8 和 39.3 个百分点。拒绝触发策略分别达到 35.6% 和 48.6%，且无论是否发生打断，所有试验均被评分。部分条件下 FLM-Audio 的有害响应率上升，而 BayLing-Duplex 则下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果确认口语打断是一种越狱攻击向量，表明需要在全双工交互的整个过程中持续进行安全评估与防护。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models accept user speech while generating responses, creating an underexplored attack surface. We introduce DuplexJail, which delivers fixed, request-independent spoken prompts through the user audio channel. We compare fixed-delay interruption after the harmful request ends with refusal-triggered interruption following a cue in the model's streaming text. Across four open-source models and 720 harmful requests from AdvBench and HarmBench, fixed-delay interruption raises whole-response attack success rates on AdvBench to 40.3% for PersonaPlex and 48.7% for PersonaPlex-RL, increases of +33.8 and +39.3 percentage points. The refusal-triggered policy reaches 35.6% and 48.6%, respectively, with all trials scored regardless of whether an interruption occurs. Selected conditions also increase FLM-Audio's harmful-response rate, while BayLing-Duplex shows decreases. These findings identify spoken interruption as a jailbreak attack vector and motivate evaluating safety throughout ongoing full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [228] [FPGA Acceleration of Fully Homomorphic Encryption with Adaptive Key Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09423)
*Zhihan Xu,Jayashree Adivarahan,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor K. Prasanna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种内存高效的KLSS数据通路，并构建性能模型对比KLSS与HKS的开销，进而设计了可在计算过程中动态切换两种密钥切换方法的自适应FPGA全同态加密加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）支持隐私保护的云服务，但计算开销巨大，需硬件加速。密钥切换是FHE的主要性能瓶颈。新型KLSS方法虽降低了部分运算复杂度，但对计算精度要求更高，与传统HKS方法形成不同权衡，其相对延迟高度依赖硬件并行度、安全参数和片上内存容量，尤其在FPGA上需谨慎平衡内存与并行度。

Method: 首先提出一种消除片外密文传输的内存高效KLSS数据通路；随后建立性能模型分析与对比KLSS和HKS的开销；基于该模型设计自适应FPGA全同态加密加速器，在计算过程中动态选择HKS或KLSS；在Alveo U280上实现并在多个FHE基准上评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，与当前最先进的FPGA加速器相比，该自适应方案在自举（bootstrapping）延迟上实现1.84-3.31倍加速，在安全图像分类上实现1.66-2.52倍加速。

Conclusion: 自适应地在HKS与KLSS之间切换可取得比单一静态方法更低的整体延迟，验证了内存高效数据通路与性能模型引导设计的有效性。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables privacy-preserving cloud services but incurs substantial computation overhead, making hardware acceleration essential. Among FHE operations, key-switching is a major performance bottleneck. Recent cryptographic advances introduce a novel key-switching method (i.e., KLSS) that reduces certain operational complexity but demands higher computational precision than the traditional Hybrid Key Switching (HKS) method. This trade-off leads to distinct computation and memory requirements, making the relative latency of KLSS and HKS highly dependent on hardware parallelism, FHE security parameters, and available on-chip memory capacity, particularly on FPGA platforms, where memory resources and parallelism must be carefully balanced.
  In this work, we first propose a memory-efficient KLSS datapath that eliminates off-chip ciphertext transfers. We then develop a performance model to analyze and compare the overheads of both KLSS and HKS. Our analysis reveals that an adaptive solution supporting both methods can achieve lower overall latency than a static method during FHE computation. Guided by the performance model, we design an adaptive FPGA-based FHE accelerator that dynamically selects between HKS and KLSS during computation. We implement the accelerator on an Alveo U280 and evaluate it across multiple FHE benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our adaptive solution achieves a 1.84-3.31$\times$ speedup in bootstrapping latency and a 1.66-2.52$\times$ speedup in secure image classification compared to state-of-the-art FPGA accelerators.

</details>


### [229] [Concept drift mitigation through community and spectral graph analysis for the detectionof cyberattacks in network traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09442)
*Julien Michel,Abdul Qadir Khan,Majed Jaber,Pierre Parrend*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出t-robustness，一种与检测模型无关、可跨特征空间比较的特征稳定性评分，将干预点从模型转移到特征选择阶段，以应对网络流量中的概念漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流量中合法行为与攻击技术共同演化（概念漂移），导致检测器在两次更新之间即失效，始终落后于对手。传统方法在漂移发生后修复模型，属于被动应对。

Method: 提出t-robustness稳定性评分，针对每个特征独立定义、独立于任何检测模型，可跨整个特征空间比较。该评分结合特征相邻统计状态之间的逐步距离，以及其相对初始状态的累积散度，避免缓慢单调漂移被误判为稳定。候选特征来自扫描、DoS及端点间通信所留下的异常网络连接模式，通过图社区度量和谱度量提取。

Result: 在UGR16数据集上、三种学习场景加一个对照场景以及无模型更新的条件下评估，t-robust特征空间在基线崩溃时仍能维持检测能力：最后一个测试区间的保留期望值为0.6025，而图社区特征为0.5230，基础NetFlow特征仅为0.3831。

Conclusion: 将干预点前移至特征空间选择可提升检测器对概念漂移的鲁棒性，t-robust特征空间在检测持续性上显著优于图社区特征和基础NetFlow特征。

Abstract: In network traffic, legitimate behaviours and attack techniques evolve jointly - the phenomenon known as 'concept drift' [1]. Every detector is thereby left obsolete between two updates, and always one step behind adversaries. In this work, we propose to move the point of intervention from the model, repaired after the drift, to the feature space, selected before learning. We therefore introduce t-robustness, a stability score defined for each feature independently of any detection model, comparable across an entire feature space. It combines the step-by-step distance between successive statistical states of a feature, and its cumulative divergence from its initial state, so that a slow monotonic drift cannot pass for stability. The candidates are drawn from abnormal network connectivity patterns left by scans, DoS and communications between endpoints, read through graph community metrics and spectral metrics. The evaluation is performed on the UGR16 dataset, across three learning scenarios and a control scenario, as well as without model update, and demonstrate that t-robust feature spaces sustain detection where the baselines collapse: retained expectancy at the last test interval reaches 0.6025, against 0.5230 for graph community features and 0.3831 for the base NetFlow features.

</details>


### [230] [Audio Deepfake Detection Using Temporal Coherence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09489)
*Justin D. Norman,Sarah Barrington*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CLAP嵌入的时间连贯性分析框架，用轻量集成分类器区分真实与AI合成音频，并揭示两个实证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成音频（深度伪造音频）对信息完整性构成威胁，如语音克隆欺诈和合成音乐版权纠纷。现有常见深度学习方法缺乏可解释性且计算开销较大，因此需要一种可解释、计算高效的替代方案，并能覆盖语音、器乐及带人声音乐等多种音频域。

Method: 基于对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）嵌入构建时间连贯性分析框架。通过计算音频片段嵌入之间的两两余弦相似度，并从所得相似度分布中提取统计特征，在此基础上训练轻量级集成分类器，用于区分真实与合成音频。

Result: 在语音和音乐域均取得有竞争力的性能。此外揭示两个实证发现：（1）特征-标签反转现象，29个统计特征中有21个在训练与真实部署之间发生判别方向反转；（2）语音-音乐方向反转，即熵对语音和音乐深度伪造的判别方向相反。

Conclusion: 该框架为音频深度伪造检测提供了一种可解释、计算高效的方案，并能跨语音与音乐域工作；同时其揭示的反转现象提示训练环境与真实部署之间存在分布差异，需引起注意。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated audio (so-called "deepfake" audio) poses significant threats to information integrity, from voice cloning fraud to synthetic music copyright disputes. We present a temporal coherence analysis framework built upon Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) embeddings that spans speech, instrumental music, and music with vocals. By computing pairwise cosine similarities between audio segment embeddings and extracting statistical features from the resulting distributions, we train lightweight ensemble classifiers that reliably distinguish authentic from synthetic audio. Our work provides an interpretable, computationally efficient alternative to common deep learning methods while still achieving competitive performance across speech and music domains. Further, we reveal two notable empirical findings about audio deepfakes: (1) a feature-label inversion phenomenon in which 21 of 29 statistical features reverse their discriminative direction between training and in-the-wild deployment, and (2) a speech--music direction reversal in which entropy discriminates in opposite directions for speech and music deepfakes.

</details>


### [231] [An Efficient and Effective Agentic Group Shilling Attack on Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09551)
*Quoc Viet Nguyen,Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Bay Vo,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AGAS——一种基于多智能体协作的角色切换式shilling攻击框架，在相同预算下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统深度依赖用户交互，易受shilling攻击（注入虚假画像操纵排序与曝光）。现有攻击依赖针对特定目标的微调或固定画像模板，难以适配不同受害模型且易被检测。

Method: AGAS由中央Coordinator指挥一组可角色切换的worker agent协同运作：Coordinator在进展停滞或抑制信号增强时动态调整策略；worker共享同一目标并在活跃/非活跃角色间切换，以避免重复模式，从而自适应地推广目标物品。

Result: 在相同攻击预算与评测协议下，AGAS在目标推广上持续超越强基线，同时更好保持良性推荐质量、削弱代表性检测器，并比既有攻击更高效。

Conclusion: 研究表明，防御推荐系统可能需要应对自适应shilling活动的机制，而非仅针对孤立的虚假画像注入。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recommender systems have become core infrastructure for modern online platforms, personalizing content at scale and strongly influencing what users see, click on, and purchase. However, this dependence on user interaction also exposes them to shilling attacks, where malicious actors can inject fake profiles to distort item rankings and control visibility. Existing attacks often rely on target-specific fine-tuning or fixed profile templates, making them either difficult to adapt to different victims or easier to detect. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Agentic Group Attack System (AGAS), a coordinated shilling framework where a central Coordinator directs a group of role-switching worker agents to adaptively promote a target item across different victim families. The Coordinator dynamically adjusts the strategy when progress stalls or suppression signals increase, while workers pursue a shared objective and switch between active and inactive roles to avoid repetitive patterns. Under the same attack budgets and evaluation protocols, AGAS consistently surpasses strong baselines in target promotion while better preserving benign recommendation quality, weakening representative detectors, and achieving higher efficiency than prior attacks. These findings also emphasize that defending recommender systems may require mechanisms that can handle adaptive shilling campaigns, not just isolated fake-profile injections. Our code is available at https://github.com/phkhanhtrinh23/AGAS.

</details>


### [232] [Arbitrary Cipher Attacks Against Large Language Models Do Not Require Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09553)
*Thomas Rivasseau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对前沿大语言模型的新型越狱攻击：模型无需微调，仅通过提示和上下文学习即可掌握密文通信技能，并借助加密通道绕过对齐与有害性分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于任意密文/隐蔽通信攻击的研究主要针对商业模型的微调API，需要先对目标模型进行微调训练。作者希望探究：更新一代的前沿模型是否无需微调即可获得基于密文的通信能力，从而构成新的攻击面。

Method: 作者不再依赖对模型微调API的训练，而是通过提示（prompting）以及在必要时通过上下文学习（in-context learning），让前沿模型自行学会并使用一套加密通信方案来进行交互。

Result: 实验表明，前沿模型无需微调即可学会密文通信技能；当通信通过所学密文进行时，模型对齐被显著削弱甚至完全绕过。作者在Anthropic、Google和OpenAI开发的前沿模型上成功实现了越狱，且由于有害内容被加密，攻击可绕过商业有害性分类器。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种针对商业黑盒大语言模型的新型攻击向量，说明仅凭提示与上下文学习即可获得密文通信能力并绕过安全对齐，对现有人工智能安全防护构成新的挑战。

Abstract: Large language model safety and security research is preoccupied with, among other things, detecting and preventing jailbreak attacks: alignment bypasses that allow an adversarial user to elicit unwanted or harmful outputs from models. Arbitrary cipher, or covert communication, attacks are one such type of jailbreak and have previously been demonstrated against the fine-tuning APIs of commercial models. In these attacks, target models are trained on a corpus of encrypted harmful questions and responses and subsequently respond to harmful requests through the learned encryption scheme. In this paper, we show that newer frontier models do not require fine-tuning to acquire cipher-based communication skills. Instead, they can learn these skills through prompting and, when necessary, through in-context learning. Furthermore, model alignment is significantly weakened or entirely bypassed when communication occurs through the learned cipher. To the best of our knowledge, this constitutes a novel attack vector against commercial black-box large language models. We demonstrate successful jailbreaks against frontier models developed by Anthropic, Google, and OpenAI. Our attack bypasses commercial harmfulness classifiers because harmful content is encrypted and therefore appears as nonsensical text or gibberish.

</details>


### [233] [When Ad Networks Misbehave: Understanding Risks of Semi-Drive-By Splash Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09574)
*Song Wu,Bo Wang,Yifan Zhang,Yinfeng Cao,Xueqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了广告网络层面的一种新型欺诈——半被动触发开屏广告（semi-drive-by splash ads），并设计自动化蜜罐框架 AdHive 在真实设备条件下诱导其暴露，大规模测量证实该欺诈普遍存在，并与广告主合作造成约400万元人民币赔付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往移动广告欺诈研究多聚焦发布商侧或内嵌广告库，而广告SDK如何解释、测量与上报交互信号这一环节风险被忽视；偶发触碰与传感器回调易被错误归因为有效交互，形成隐蔽的计费欺诈空间。

Method: 设计 AdHive 自动化类蜜罐分析框架，通过 LLM 生成类人使用轨迹与传感器动态，在真实设备条件下复现人类活动，诱导规避式开屏广告投递与落地行为，从而暴露常规分析环境中隐藏的执行路径。

Result: 对数千款热门 Android 应用的大规模测量显示，半被动触发开屏广告普遍存在，常由细微传感器变化等信号触发；与中国某大型广告主合作确认多家广告网络参与该欺诈，促成约400万元人民币（约60万美元）的强制赔付。

Conclusion: 研究表明广告网络层面存在被忽视的开屏广告欺诈风险，AdHive 框架可有效在真实环境中揭露此类规避行为，并已产生可量化的实际经济影响，提示需加强对交互信号归因与广告SDK上报机制的审计与监管。

Abstract: We investigate the mobile splash ads ecosystem, i.e., full-screen advertisements shown at app launch, where monetization relies on interaction signals that are difficult to verify end-to-end. This setting is especially sensitive because incidental touches and sensor-driven callbacks are common yet easy to misattribute as engagement. Prior work has largely framed mobile ad fraud as a publisher-side problem, while some studies attribute fraudulent operations to embedded ad libraries. Yet an important risk remains underexplored: ad SDKs control how interaction signals are interpreted, measured, and reported, creating an opportunity to reinterpret ambiguous user or device signals as valid advertising interactions. We uncover a previously less-known form of fraud at the ad-network layer in which splash ads are triggered not by intentional user actions but by incidental or indirect interactions, which we term semi-drive-by splash ads. By translating non-ad interactions into billable engagement events, ad networks can inflate performance metrics, overcharge advertisers, and erode user trust. To expose this behavior in the wild, we design AdHive, an automated honeypot-like analysis framework that induces evasive splash-ad delivery and landing behaviors under realistic device conditions. AdHive reproduces human-like activity through LLM-generated usage traces and sensor dynamics, enabling execution paths that remain hidden in conventional analysis environments.
  Our large-scale measurement across thousands of popular Android applications shows that semi-drive-by splash ads are widespread and are often triggered by subtle signals such as minor sensor variations. We further confirm real-world impact by working with one of China's largest advertisers, identifying multiple ad networks engaging in this fraud and leading to enforced repayments of about 4 million Yuan (approximately US$600,000).

</details>


### [234] [Scalable Composition of Byzantine Agreements under Reorder Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09623)
*Jing Chen,Jin Dong,Jichen Li,Xuanzhi Xia,Wentao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出结合节点腐败与信道攻击的敌手模型，为并行/并发组合下的拜占庭协议建立了紧致的安全阈值，并给出了匹配的不可能性与可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭协议（BA）的多实例执行安全性分析多只关注节点腐败，忽略了攻击者操纵通信信道带来的威胁。通过信道攻击，消息可在多个执行间被重排，从而破坏协议的安全保证。因此需要一个新的敌手模型来同时刻画节点腐败与信道攻击。

Method: 提出首个同时结合节点腐败与信道攻击的敌手模型；基于该模型推导并行与并发组合下的新安全阈值，给出互补且相互匹配的不可能性与可能性结果。对于可能性结果，先提供通用黑盒编译器，将任意单实例安全BA协议转化为并行/并发执行下安全的协议（无需额外安全假设）；再利用纠删码设计改进编译器以优化性能。

Result: 不可能性：即使认证拜占庭协议，在并行组合下当 n ≤ 3t 或 n ≤ 2c + 2t + 1 时也不安全（t 为腐败节点数，c 为通信信道数，n 为节点数）。可能性：当 n > max{3t, 2c+2t+1} 时，存在非认证拜占庭协议在并行与并发组合下安全。改进编译器显著降低通信开销，对长消息实现常数倍开销，达到与原协议相同的渐近通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了结合节点腐败与信道攻击的统一敌手模型，并给出并行/并发组合下拜占庭协议的紧致安全界 n > max{3t, 2c+2t+1}，同时通过黑盒与纠删码编译器实现高效的安全协议构造。

Abstract: Byzantine agreement (BA) is a foundational building block in distributed systems, and the security analysis of BA protocols under multi-instance executions has attracted increasing attention. However, most existing adversary models focus solely on party corruption and neglect important threats posed by adversarial manipulations of communication channels in the network. Through channel attacks, messages can be reordered across multiple executions and lead to violations of the protocol's security guarantees,
  In this work, we present the first adversary model that combines party corruption and channel attacks. Based on this model, we establish new security thresholds for Byzantine agreement under parallel and concurrent compositions, supported by complementary impossibility and possibility results that match each other to form a tight bound. For the impossibility result, we show that even authenticated Byzantine agreement protocols cannot be secure under parallel composition when $n \leq 3t$ or $n \leq 2c + 2t + 1$, where $t$ and $c$ denote the number of corrupted parties and communication channels, respectively, and $n$ is the number of parties.
  For the possibility result, we prove the existence of secure protocols for unauthenticated Byzantine agreement under parallel and concurrent composition, when $n > \max\{3t, 2c+2t+1\}$. We first provide general black-box compilers that transform any single-instance secure BA protocol into one that is secure under parallel and concurrent executions without additional security assumptions. To optimize performance, we further design refined compilers using erasure-correcting codes. These refined versions significantly reduce communication overhead, particularly for long messages, where they achieve a constant multiplicative overhead compared with the original protocol, thus achieving the same asymptotic communication complexity.

</details>


### [235] [Session Attestation for Unmodified TLS Services in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09668)
*Qi Gu,Sheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SessionLatch在操作系统层实现会话证明，保持应用、TLS实现与证书不变，通过可信观测服务器本地生成的临时公钥并借助标准TLS密钥确认建立TEE端点保证，无需访问TLS密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机便于将现有服务迁入可信执行环境，但对其网络连接进行证明通常需修改应用、TLS实现或证书，阻碍落地。

Method: 核心是可信观测服务器本地生成的临时公钥并结合标准TLS密钥确认，建立TEE端点保证而不访问TLS密钥；将证明集成下移到操作系统：临时锁存（latch）暂存客户端加密记录，证据交换与应用TLS握手重叠，验证后自动移除。双向证明采用相同构造并重叠两端证据生成。实现了Linux与Windows集成，并基于真实Hygon CSV证明进行评估。

Result: 在Linux/Windows交错实验中，相比TNG，短上传平均延迟降低63.1%/23.0%。

Conclusion: 会话证明可在不将永久代理置于数据路径中的前提下，增强现有机密服务的安全性。

Abstract: Confidential virtual machines simplify the migration of existing services into trusted execution environments, yet attesting their network connections often requires changing applications, TLS implementations, or certificates. We present SessionLatch, which provides session attestation while preserving all three. The key insight is that a trusted observation of the server's locally generated ephemeral public key, combined with standard TLS key confirmation, establishes the TEE endpoint guarantee without accessing TLS secrets. This moves attestation integration to the operating system: a temporary latch holds client encrypted records while evidence exchange overlaps the application TLS handshake, then removes itself after verification. The resulting connection retains enterprise service authentication and the native TLS data path, with no additional payload encryption. Mutual attestation uses the same construction and overlaps evidence generation at both endpoints. We implement Linux andWindowsintegrationandevaluaterealHygonCSVattestation. SessionLatch reduces short-upload mean latency by 63.1%/23.0% relative to TNG in interleaved Linux/Windows experiments. These results show that session attestation can strengthen existing confidential services without making a permanent proxy part of their data path.

</details>


### [236] [Automating Static Code Analysis Through CI/CD Pipeline Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00676)
*Zachary Wadhams,Ann Marie Reinhold,Clemente Izurieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用、自动化的SAST工具输出聚合流程，将结果集成到开发者熟悉的issue跟踪系统中，并以SonarQube+GitLab环境验证其可行性，开发者反馈积极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护敏感数据对组织声誉、财务安全和个人隐私至关重要。SAST工具虽能检测漏洞，但因高误报率、缺乏原生流水线支持等可用性问题，导致其广泛采用受阻，亟需在开发早期高效识别和修复安全漏洞。

Method: 提出一种新颖、通用且自动化的流程，用于聚合SAST工具的输出，并将其集成到开发者熟悉的issue跟踪软件中，从而在开发生命周期中简化漏洞识别与沟通。在基于GitLab的开发环境中使用SonarQube工具进行了实现与演示。

Result: 成功实现该流程。开发者对结构化实现、实时反馈和主动漏洞管理持正面态度；但也存在潜在学习曲线以及安全编码与工作流干扰之间的权衡等挑战。整体上对安全意识和响应能力产生了积极影响。

Conclusion: 该自动化聚合与集成流程在提升软件开发安全态势方面具有良好前景，尽管仍需应对学习成本和流程干扰等权衡问题。

Abstract: In the contemporary landscape of software devel-opment, securing sensitive data is paramount to safeguarding organizational reputation, preventing financial losses, and pro-tecting individuals from identity theft. This paper addresses the pervasive challenge of identifying and rectifying security vulnerabilities early in the development process, emphasizing the role of Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools. While SAST tools play a crucial role in detecting vulnerabilities, widespread adoption has been hindered by usability issues, including high false positive rates and a lack of native pipeline support. This paper proposes a novel, generalized, and automated process for aggregating SAST tool outputs and integrating them into developers' familiar issue-tracking software. The process streamlines the identification and communication of security vulnerabilities during the development lifecycle, facilitating more efficient remediation efforts. We demonstrate the successful implementation of the proposed process with the SonarQube SAST tool in a GitLab-based development environment. Developers were positive about the structured implementation, real-time feedback, and proactive vulnerability management. However, despite some challenges such as a potential learning curve and tradeoffs between secure coding and workflow disruption, the overall positive impact on security awareness and responsiveness suggests that the proposed process holds promise in enhancing the security posture of software development practices

</details>


### [237] [PrivAudit: A Dual-Lens Auditing Framework for Website Privacy Practices under the CCPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09697)
*Mohamed Moustafa Dawoud,Riya Aggarwal,Likith Rahul Krishnamurthy,Ram Sundara Raman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrivAudit 自动化审计框架，采用"双镜头"方法，结合基于大语言模型的隐私政策分析与浏览器端 cookie 写入测量，对 998 个网站进行大规模审计，检验 CCPA 的实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCPA 实施五年后，网站隐私实践如何在大规模上随监管演变仍不清楚。既有工作和监管执法多依赖人工、个案式审查，缺乏可扩展方法来系统审计网站的两个关键面向：隐私披露与前端用户追踪行为。

Method: PrivAudit 采用双镜头方法：(1) 以 CCPA 条款为基础，用大语言模型分析隐私政策中的隐私披露；(2) 通过自动化浏览器在不同隐私配置下测量 cookie 写入，捕捉用户可观测的数据收集行为。随后将该框架应用于 998 个网站。

Result: 法律与更强的隐私披露相关：受 CCPA 约束的政策更可能披露退出机制、数据共享实践和用户权利。但基于 cookie 的追踪依然普遍，受约束与不受约束的网站共设置 6,392 个定向 cookie，其中 49% 为第三方写入。此外，cookie 对隐私信号和同意选择的响应仅为有限到中等程度，即便网站在披露中声称会遵守这些选择。

Conclusion: 结果凸显需要多层次、可扩展的审计方法，将政策分析与行为证据相结合。PrivAudit 可通过生成可操作信号与模式支持大规模审计工作流，供进一步人工复核。作者已开源 PrivAudit，并正与监管机构合作以支持实际审计。

Abstract: Five years after the enforcement of the California Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA), understanding how website privacy practices evolve at scale in response to regulation remains a key challenge for both researchers and regulators. Prior work and regulatory efforts have focused on manual and case-specific enforcement, but there remain no scalable approaches to systematically audit two key user-facing facets of websites that are crucial signals for the CCPA: privacy disclosures and front-end user tracking behavior.
  In this paper, we present PrivAudit, an automated auditing framework that adopts a dual-lens approach to capture: (1) privacy disclosures through large language model-based analysis of privacy policies grounded in CCPA provisions, and (2) user-observable data collection behavior through automated browser measurements of cookie writes under diverse privacy configurations. We apply PrivAudit to 998 websites and report two broad findings. The law is associated with stronger privacy disclosures: CCPA-subject policies are more likely to disclose opt-out mechanisms, data-sharing practices, and user rights. On the other hand, cookie-based tracking remains pervasive, with both CCPA-subject and not-subject websites setting a total of 6,392 targeting cookies, 49% of which are third-party writes. Moreover, cookies show limited-to-moderate responsiveness to privacy signals and consent choices, even when websites claim to honor them in their disclosures.
  Our results highlight the need for multi-layered and scalable auditing approaches that combine policy analysis with behavioral evidence. PrivAudit can support these auditing workflows at scale by generating actionable signals and patterns for further manual review. We open-source PrivAudit and are engaging with regulators to support auditing in practice.

</details>


### [238] [How Fragile Is Safety Alignment at Frontier Scale? A Single-Direction Attack on a 320B MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09793)
*Yi Shi,Tanyu Chen,Kai Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 定向消融在320B参数的MoE前沿模型GLM-5.3-Flash上依然有效，但效果分布与稠密模型完全不同：单模块编辑收益极小，74%的拒绝能力消除效应仅在注意力、稠密与路由专家三类写入者联合干预时出现，传统按模块名匹配的方法会静默失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向消融此前仅在大约70B参数以内的稠密模型上得到验证。而前沿MoE模型的残差流不再是单一张量、权重以量化形式发布，因此有必要检验该白盒攻击是否仍适用于此类架构，并厘清其作用位置是否发生迁移。

Method: 将定向消融应用于GLM-5.3-Flash（320B参数、288个路由专家、四宽超连接残差、block-FP8），无需梯度训练或优化，仅用数百条对比提示。分别及联合编辑注意力、稠密层和路由专家三类写入者，并配合随机正交方向对照、按模块名匹配的常规配方，以及在暴力、色情、仇恨内容上拟合的1至12秩子空间进行检验。

Result: 单独编辑注意力、稠密与路由专家分别消除0.039、0.016和0.148的拒绝能力，三者联合编辑则消除0.776，即74%的效应只在联合干预下存在；按模块名匹配的常规配方仅覆盖其中0.066。消融随机正交方向不改变拒绝行为。在暴力、色情和仇恨内容上拟合的子空间在秩1至12的每一档都保留了可测量的拒绝残留。在七个有害基准上实现41至89个百分点的下降，且未检测到能力变化。

Conclusion: 定向消融能够跨越MoE架构与量化权重的转变而存续，但其作用位点已偏移，单一模块编辑与基于模块名的传统定位方式在MoE上会静默失效，必须进行跨写入者的联合干预才能获得主要效果；同时存在一类按类别集中、对各类编辑均具鲁棒性的拒绝残留，界定了该方法的能力边界。

Abstract: Directional ablation removes an aligned language model's ability to refuse by projecting a single "refusal direction" out of the weights that write the residual stream. It needs no gradient-based training and no optimization, only a few hundred contrastive prompts, which makes it the canonical white-box attack on open-weight alignment. However, it has been established only on dense models up to roughly 70B parameters. We study whether it survives the shift to frontier mixture-of-experts (MoE) models whose residual streams are no longer a single tensor and whose weights ship quantized. We apply it to GLM-5.3-Flash (320B parameters, 288 routed experts, a four-wide hyper-connection residual, block-FP8). The attack survives the architecture, but what it reaches is no longer where a reader of the original recipe would look for it. Editing the attention, dense and routed-expert writers on their own removes 0.039, 0.016 and 0.148 of refusal respectively; editing all three together removes 0.776. As a result, 74% of the effect exists only under the joint intervention. The part the conventional recipe reaches by module-name matching accounts for 0.066 of that 0.776, which is why it fails silently on an MoE. The effect does not follow from removing just any direction: ablating a random direction orthogonal to it leaves refusal unchanged. A category-concentrated residue survives every edit we tried: subspaces fitted on violence, sexual content and hate leave measurable refusal at every rank from 1 to 12. We report the method, the 41-89 percentage-point reductions it achieves across seven harmful benchmarks with no detected change in capability, and the boundary where it stops.

</details>


### [239] [TrajMark: Ownership Attribution and Segment-Level Tamper Localization for Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10416)
*Bokang Zeng,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajMark 是一个免训练、对称密钥、仅可见的轨迹水印框架，将鲁棒的所有权归属与脆弱的局部完整性验证分离，用于编码智能体轨迹的溯源与篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对编码智能体最终补丁加水印只能提供产物溯源，无法认证产生该产物的可见过程；现有行为水印方法仅提供全局检测或标识符恢复信号，局部编辑的轨迹可能仍保留归属证据，却无法揭示哪个受保护区域已变得不一致。

Method: TrajMark 采用两层互补结构：(1) 稀疏所有者层，通过将自然出现的 READ 动作的键控子集改写为带掩码的线性方程，编码六位部署标识符；(2) 定位层，插入相互关联的 Q12 普通、分组和终止封印，以承诺受保护的关键动作段。文中还提供了关于所有者可恢复性、完整性碰撞概率、结构开销和定位行为的设计级分析。

Result: 在三种编码智能体框架和三种 LLM 上，TrajMark 在所有评估的干净全水印批次中均恢复出准确所有者；在穷举的合格单点攻击下检测到 95.5%-100% 的编辑；在随机单动作破坏下，将 95.8% 的修改位点定位到可接受的协议区域而非单个动作。所有者标记不增加轨迹动作；完整性层增加显式的只读封印；匹配的 Pass@1 为 26.9%，而未加水印运行为 26.3%。

Conclusion: TrajMark 通过分离鲁棒归属与脆弱完整性验证，实现了编码智能体轨迹的所有权认证与局部篡改定位，且几乎不影响任务性能，为智能体过程级溯源提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Watermarking the final patch produced by a coding agent provides provenance evidence for the submitted artifact, but does not authenticate the visible process that produced it. Behavioral watermarking methods primarily provide a global detection or identifier-recovery signal, so a locally edited trajectory may retain sufficient ownership evidence without revealing which protected region has become inconsistent. To address this limitation, we propose TrajMark, a training-free, symmetric-key, visible-only trajectory watermarking framework that separates robust ownership attribution from fragile local integrity verification. Our framework consists of two complementary layers: a sparse owner layer that encodes a six-bit deployment identifier by rewriting a keyed subset of naturally occurring READ actions into masked linear equations, and a localization layer that inserts linked Q12 ordinary, group, and terminal seals to commit to protected critical-action segments. This separation allows ownership evidence to accumulate robustly across trajectories, while local modifications perturb nearby keyed commitments and expose the affected protocol region. We further provide a design-level analysis of owner recoverability, integrity collision probability, structural overhead, and localization behavior. Across three coding-agent frameworks and three LLMs, TrajMark recovers the exact owner in all evaluated clean full-watermark batches. Under exhaustive eligible single-site attacks it detects 95.5%-100% of edits, and under random single-action corruption it localizes 95.8% of modified sites to an accepted protocol region rather than to the individual action. Owner marking adds no trajectory actions; the integrity layer adds explicit read-only seals, and matched Pass@1 is 26.9% versus 26.3% for unwatermarked runs.

</details>


### [240] [CS-Guard: Benchmarking LLM Guardrails for Code Generation Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09798)
*Jinyang Li,Mingyu Guo,Hung X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CS-Guard，首个系统性评估代码生成安全防护栏（guardrails）的基准，覆盖文本到代码和代码到代码两类生成任务，并在7个LLM上评估9种防护栏，发现现有防护栏对恶意代码生成请求的防御效果普遍较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已被用于生成恶意软件，但针对代码生成安全的防护栏有效性尚不明确，缺乏系统性评估手段。

Method: 构建 CS-Guard 基准：1）文本到代码任务，包含1000个高质量恶意软件生成提示、7种越狱攻击及一种新提出的虚构场景攻击（FSA）；2）代码到代码任务，包含331个代码提示，覆盖代码填充、代码补全和代码翻译。在7个LLM上实测9种防护栏，并采用模块化三层防护栏分类法支持开发者注册防护栏进行评估。

Result: 现有防护栏对恶意代码生成请求防御效果差：文本到代码场景下，越狱后平均攻击成功率（ASR）在多种防护栏上约为50%；代码到代码场景下，基础LLM上平均ASR接近100%，多数防护栏下仍保持14.4%至接近100%的高水平；FSA在多种防护栏上ASR接近100%。

Conclusion: 现有代码生成安全防护栏在真实软件开发场景中可靠性存疑，需进一步研究改进；CS-Guard基准与数据已公开，供社区后续评估使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been ex- ploited to generate malware, but the effective- ness of guardrails for code generation secu- rity remains unclear. We introduce CS-Guard, the first benchmark to systematically evalu- ate guardrails for code generation security. It covers 1) text-to-code generation with 1000 high-quality malware-generation prompts, 7 jailbreak attacks, and a novel fictional scenario attack (FSA) that embeds malicious intent in a legitimate fictional software-development sce- nario; and 2) code-to-code generation with 331 code prompts spanning code infilling, code completion, and code translation. We empiri- cally evaluate 9 guardrails across seven LLMs. We find that current guardrails perform poorly against malicious code-generation re- quests: for text-to-code, the average attack success rate (ASR) after jailbreaks reaches about 50% for many guardrails; for code-to- code, average ASR approaches 100% on base LLMs and remains high across many guardrails (14.4% to nearly 100%). Our FSA also achieves ASR close to 100% across many guardrails, raising major reliability concerns for real-world software development. To sup- port future research, CS-Guard uses a modular three-layer guardrail taxonomy that lets devel- opers register guardrails for evaluation. We release the benchmark and data to enable fur- ther community evaluation.

</details>


### [241] [Wicked Problem, Parsimonious Solution: Securing Electric Vehicle Charging Station Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10502)
*Emma Sheppard,Zachary Wadhams,Dalton Arford,Clemente Izurieta,Ann Marie Reinhold*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文主张将分层软件质量保证（HSQA）应用于电动汽车充电站软件，以系统性地刻画并评估其代码质量与安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车充电基础设施面临新型网络物理威胁，其中充电站是最脆弱的环节。充电站供电设备中的软件尤其脆弱，目前这一攻击面大多缺乏保护且特征刻画不足，因此亟需一种能够全面表征其漏洞的方法。

Method: 作者提出倡导将现代软件质量保证方法应用于电动汽车充电站软件的漏洞刻画，具体主张采用分层软件质量保证（HSQA）。HSQA 在软件开发生命周期中融入质量与安全考量，能够提供从单个漏洞（如 CVE）到高层次特性（如 CIA 三元组）的代码质量与安全的全景视图。

Result: 本文为一篇立场性论文（position paper），未报告具体实验或量化结果，其核心产出是论证 HSQA 作为评估电动汽车充电站软件方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 作者的立场是：HSQA 是评估电动汽车充电站软件的一种优秀方法，能够全面覆盖从个体漏洞到高层安全特性的多层级质量与安全维度。

Abstract: Electric vehicle charging infrastructure presents a suite of novel cyber-physical threats. Among this infrastructure, charging stations are the most vulnerable elements. The software in the charging station supply equipment is particularly vulnerable. Currently, the software is an attack surface that is largely unprotected and poorly characterized. To represent the vulnerabilities in this attack surface, we advocate for applying modern software quality assurance to characterize vulnerabilities in electric vehicle charging station software. Specifically, we advocate for the application of hierarchical software quality assurance (HSQA) to specialized electric vehicle charging station software. HSQA provides a comprehensive view of the code quality and security -- from the level of individual vulnerabilities (e.g., CVEs) to high level characteristics (e.g., CIA Triad). HSQA incorporates quality and security considerations throughout the software development lifecycle. Thus, our position is that HSQA is an excellent approach for assessing electrical vehicle charging station software.

</details>


### [242] [Quantifying IIoT Sensor Node Criticality by Fusing its Data Criticality and Security Vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09807)
*Sachin K. Sen,Gour C. Karmakar,Shaoning Pang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性的IIoT传感器节点关键性评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT传感器节点易受网络威胁，现有研究或关注安全漏洞，或关注数据对产品质量的影响，缺乏同时整合数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性的综合评估方法。

Method: 采用Dempster-Shafer（D-S）证据理论，融合传感器数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性，构建IIoT传感器节点关键性评估框架；使用CVSS 4.0与3.1计算脆弱性评分，并以红酒生产数据集进行验证。

Result: 基于CVSS 4.0安全脆弱性评分的关键性排名与CVSS 3.1的结果存在显著差异，表明增强的脆弱性评估方法会影响节点关键性排序。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效基于数据关键性与安全脆弱性对传感器节点进行排序，并可经少量修改推广至更广泛的工业应用，为IIoT生产系统安全提供稳健方法。

Abstract: The integration of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) into manufacturing has transformed industrial operations by optimising production management and ensuring product quality through smart industrial sensors that regulate processes based on real-time data. However, these sensor nodes are highly vulnerable to cyber threats, posing significant security risks that compromise their reliability and integrity. While existing research explores cybersecurity vulnerabilities and cyberattack-based methods for ranking critical nodes, some studies assess node criticality based on the impact of sensor data on product quality. However, a comprehensive approach that integrates both data criticality and cybersecurity vulnerability remains unexplored. To bridge this gap, this study introduces a novel framework that evaluates IIoT sensor node criticality by leveraging Dempster--Shafer (D-S) theory to fuse data criticality and cybersecurity vulnerabilities. The proposed method is validated using a dataset from red wine production, demonstrating its effectiveness in ranking sensor nodes based on both factors. The results show that criticality rankings based on security vulnerability scores computed using CVSS version 4.0 differ significantly from those obtained with CVSS version 3.1, highlighting the influence of enhanced vulnerability assessment methodologies. While initially applied to wine manufacturing, this framework is adaptable to broader industrial applications with minimal modifications, offering a robust approach to securing IIoT-enabled production systems.

</details>


### [243] [Lightweight Zero Trust via Automotive SDN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09817)
*Friedrich Wiemer,Florian Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证无需新增专用基础设施，仅借助既有TC17 MACsec/MKA与TC19 CORECONF/YANG管理即可实现汽车域零信任架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域车载网络虽已部署以太网、MACsec与TSN，但网络本身被默认为可信：出厂配置后缺乏标准化的运行时手段来撤销访问、轮换密钥或遏制被入侵的ECU。现有汽车ZTA方案需额外堆叠专用基础设施，重复了SDV本就需要的SDN管理面，导致ZTA在汽车领域难以落地。

Method: 分两步：第一步分析Open Alliance TC17 v1.0的MACsec/MKA（采用预共享CAK）对NIST SP 800-207各项ZTA原则的覆盖情况；第二步引入Open Alliance TC19提出的CORECONF/YANG管理，将SDN控制器与代理一一映射到NIST的PE、PA与PEP，并以两种基于YANG的机制（网络访问控制流与密钥管理方案）进行实例化。

Result: 在不增加任何ZTA专用基础设施的前提下，完整覆盖七项ZTA原则中的五项，并部分覆盖另外两项。

Conclusion: 汽车领域可复用既有的MACsec/MKA与CORECONF/YANG管理面来实现零信任，避免了重复建设专用基础设施，为ZTA在SDV中的实际采纳提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Zonal in-vehicle networks ship Ethernet, MACsec, and TSN, but treat the network itself as trusted: once configured at the factory, there is no standardized runtime way to easily revoke access, rotate keys, or contain a compromised ECU. Zero Trust Architecture targets exactly that gap, yet existing automotive ZTA proposals bolt on dedicated infrastructure that duplicates the SDN management plane already required to enable SDVs. Thus, ZTA is not yet adopted in the automotive domain, and the question remains: can we do better? We answer this in two steps.
  Step 1 analyses what Open Alliance TC17~v1.0 MACsec/MKA with pre-shared CAKs already provides in terms of NIST SP~800-207 ZTA tenets. Step 2 adds CORECONF/YANG management as proposed in Open Alliance TC19, maps the SDN Controller and Agents one-to-one onto NIST's PE, PA, and PEP. We then instantiate this with two YANG-based mechanisms: a network-access-control flow and a key-management scheme.
  The result fully covers five and two partially of the seven tenets with no ZTA-specific infrastructure added.

</details>


### [244] [Subgroup Membership Inference Audits of Differentially Private Synthetic Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09848)
*Yidan Sun,Viktor Schlegel,Srinivasan Nandakumar,Siew Kei Lam,Anil Anthony Bharath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 合成数据发布存在残余隐私风险，现有成员推理审计只测平均风险，会掩盖脆弱子群的风险。本文提出面向子群目标的成员推理博弈，在32个代理、4个数据集、3种生成器、5个隐私预算下审计，发现合成发布确实泄漏子群成员信息，且DP的残余泄漏高度集中、保护不均，哪些记录泄漏取决于发布机制而非记录本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据发布被当作敏感数据集的替代共享方案，即使有DP约束最坏情况泄漏，实际仍存在残余风险。现有MIA审计仅针对随机抽取记录测平均风险，可能掩盖脆弱子群的泄漏风险。

Method: 定义子群目标成员推理博弈，将目标池作为显式参数；在32个代理、三种攻击者知识场景、四个数据集、三种生成器（DP-SGD微调、API提示、激活引导）和五个隐私预算下进行审计。

Result: 合成发布泄漏子群成员信息，既有攻击系统性低估该泄漏；DP在聚合层面有效，各预算下显著降低平均泄漏。但三点值得注意：残余泄漏集中（十分之一记录约承担40%）；DP保护不均，随机记录被去噪多于高风险记录，合并池审计在记录级证实；哪些记录泄漏取决于发布机制而非记录本身。

Conclusion: 记录级风险不能脱离发布机制独立评估，需关注脆弱子群和泄漏集中性，重新审视DP合成数据的实证隐私评估方式。

Abstract: Synthetic data releases are increasingly proposed in the literature as a means of sharing realistic data replicas in lieu of sensitive private datasets.
